### 261 subject events

- **1914.07** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.08** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1914.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1915.04** `statistical_infection`

- **1915.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1915.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Klara踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Klara抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Klara举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Emil Weiss把Klara拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Klara把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg门口，Klara蹲下把两包并排，按Emil Weiss说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; skill_

- **1918.04** `r1-child.first-day-certificate::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement::rural-first-day/rural-barn-classroom` tags: school, first_day, bureaucracy, poverty, childhood, rural, agriculture, small_town
  教室的屋顶漏雨，课桌是用木板架在砖上的。Emil Weiss 2把Klara安排在炉子旁边，说新来的先管火。Klara一整天都在添柴，只学会了一个字：火。放学时Emil Weiss 2说，明天教你写自己的名字。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_school_stove; skill; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1918.07** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  煤车刚过去，路心还留着几块碎煤。Klara的铁环撞上去，弹到水沟边。Anna Berg先一步把铁环踩住，说要拿一颗石弹子来换。Klara蹲下去掏口袋，膝盖在石板棱上蹭掉一块皮，换回铁环时裤腿已经渗出血点；Klara没再争，只记住Anna Berg踩铁环的那只脚。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood -0.04; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1918.07** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/saw-the-bundle` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, rural, agriculture
  地窖门虚掩着，Klara下去找腌菜坛，看见Josef Bauer蹲在煤堆后面，把一包用油纸裹好的东西塞进墙缝。Josef Bauer听见脚步，回头时脸上还带着灰；Klara说自己只是来拿坛子，声音比平时高。
  _effects: state_add iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:31; knowledge “大人在酒窖的墙缝里藏了一包用油纸裹着的东西。”; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.019999999999999997; flag +saw_cellar_bundle; flag +child_secret_active_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Emil Weiss 2让Klara站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Emil Weiss 2在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Klara把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary; health -0.010000000000000009 (cold classroom)_

  Klara把面包在汤里按了按，等Josef Bauer把话头转到别处，才敢抬头。那半句话在舌根下面发苦，像一块没有化开的药；Klara又喝了两口汤，说今天的盐放多了。Josef Bauer起身去拿水，椅子在地板上刮了一声。

- **1921.03** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/eraser-cut-in-half` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  教师儿子把Klara的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Klara没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Klara发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Maria Huber 2问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1922.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Klara的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:36; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Emil Kraus一挣，Klara只能把Emil Kraus抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Klara背着Emil Kraus在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Emil Kraus擦鼻涕。等Emil Kraus睡沉，Klara的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000726:child_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Klara的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Klara把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Grete Stein翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Klara。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1922.09** `r14-edu.humiliation::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/caned-for-answering` tags: education, school_humiliation, reputation, childhood, university, culture, schooling, school
  Klara说补丁是母亲缝的，Emil Weiss 3让Klara伸出手心，用戒尺打了三下。Maria Weiss在下面咳了一声。放学后，Klara把手泡在井水里，掌心肿得握不住笔。第二天，Klara用左手写完了听写，错了一半。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (黑板前的湿鞋印); reputation -0.05; flag +caned_for_answering_back; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Klara前面的大婶一直往后靠，Klara就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Klara的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1923.03** `r1-child.punishment-peas::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/left-hand-for-the-ruler` tags: school, punishment, teacher, dialect, authority, university, culture, schooling
  Franz Lechner举起戒尺时，Klara先把左手伸出去，说打右手明天写不了字。教室里有人笑，Franz Lechner愣了一下，尺子落在左手心上，只打了两下。那天Klara用左手抄完了五十遍，字歪得像刚学走路的笔画。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (讲台上的干豌豆); reputation 0.04; flag +offered_left_hand_to_ruler; skill; relation rival with person-franz-lechner_

- **1923.10** `r1-child.reading-timetable::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/alphabet-on-the-headboard` tags: literacy, tram, street, school, breakthrough, university, culture, schooling
  Klara把报纸上的字抄在包装纸上，贴满床头。Josef Bauer嫌费灯油，把纸撕了。Grete Stein知道后，给Klara一本缺封面的旧课本，说别贴在墙上，记在脑子里。那本书的扉页写着另一个孩子的名字，Klara用橡皮擦了很久，没擦掉。
  _effects: flag +kept_headboard_alphabet; skill; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation friend with person-grete-stein_

- **1923.12** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  “Notwendigkeit”少了一个字母，Maria Huber 2没说话，只敲了敲桌角。Klara回家时手指上全是墨水，母亲问起，只说写了点作业。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1923.12** `kernel-edu.lesson-recitation::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school::edu-recitation-medical/edu-recitation-medical-check` tags: childhood, school, lesson, teacher, punishment, medical, university, knowledge
  学校今天不发课本，改成体检。Grete Stein让全班脱鞋量身高，又排队让医生听胸口。Klara被叫作“营养不良”，名字写在一张黄纸上，Grete Stein看了一眼，没说话。那张黄纸被母亲贴在柜门内侧。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (examined at the school clinic); condition; reputation -0.03; flag +school_medical_check_

- **1924.01** `r1-child.scholarship-vetting::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/signed-the-pledge` tags: scholarship, exam, politics, class, school, university, culture, schooling
  Franz Winter把名单折起来，说只要Klara签一份“不参加校外集会”的保证书，名额照给。Klara问集会是什么样，Franz Winter说就是很多人站在一起说话。Klara签了，回家路上第一次注意到街角站着两个不说话的巡警。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +signed_apolitical_pledge; knowledge “the scholarship requires a pledge to avoid public gatherings”; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1924.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/note-under-inkwell` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Klara等到天黑，Grete Stein一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Klara把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Klara按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。
  _effects: skill_

- **1924.03** `r1-child.choir-voice-break::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/replaced-by-the-new-boy` tags: choir, confirmation, church, voice, adolescence, university, culture, schooling
  弥撒进行到一半，Klara忽然唱不出声，只张嘴。Anton Winter 2用琴声盖过去，Anna Moser回头看了一眼。散场后Anton Winter 2把Klara的独唱位置给了新来的男孩，说这不是惩罚，是嗓子到了年纪。Klara把谱子折好，放进抽屉最底层。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +replaced_as_soloist; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1924.08** `recipe-education.scholarship-exam::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/passed` tags: education, exam, class, poverty, ambition, university, culture, schooling
  Klara用Maria Weiss借的钢笔写完最后一题，笔尖在纸上划出一道细缝。放榜那天，Friedrich Kral把Klara的名字念了两遍，Maria Weiss站在人群后面，先鼓掌，再低头看自己的鞋。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1924.09** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/admonished-and-sent-home` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Franz Winter把案卷合上，问Klara知不知道错了。Klara的腿在长椅上晃着，够不着地，说“知道”。Katharina Bauer在旁边一直点头，Maria Moser把一张写着“训诫”的纸递给她。出门时，Katharina Bauer把纸折好放进内衣口袋，怕人看见。
  _effects: flag +admonished_by_juvenile_court; role; relation guardian with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the juvenile court keeps a record even without a sentence”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/teacher-explains` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在办公室门口站到下课，才敢把作业本递进去。Emil Weiss 2翻到错得最多的那一页，没有骂，只让Klara把题目再读一遍。读到第三遍，Klara忽然明白自己一直看漏了条件。Emil Weiss 2把红笔放下，说以后不懂就来。那天以后，Klara的作业本上多了几行红字，都是Emil Weiss 2夜里改的。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1925.04** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Lechner先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Klara每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:101; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.07** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Klara把手套交给Elisabeth Bauer，让Elisabeth Bauer替自己去还。Elisabeth Bauer回来时说，Josef问是谁捡到的；Elisabeth Bauer顺口报了一个名字，Klara听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:100; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.08** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Klara在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Elisabeth Huber跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Klara去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.09** `r14-edu.entrance-failure::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/passed-but-no-fee` tags: education, exam_failure, entrance_exam, shame, university, culture, schooling, student
  Klara过了录取线，学费却还差十二克朗。Josef Bauer把存折翻到最后一面，又去问邻居借，回来时只说再等等。报到日过去三天，名额给了候补。Klara把录取通知压在玻璃板下，每天写功课前都看一眼那枚红章。报名费两克朗，是母亲从菜钱里扣的。
  _effects: cash -2 (放榜墙上的空白); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +admission_fee_shortfall; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Josef。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 41 -> 46; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Klara又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/own-marks-slipped` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Klara每晚给Helene Kraus讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Klara的名次掉了十几位，Friedrich Kral在家长会上点名。回家后，Klara看见Helene Kraus趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +neglected_own_studies; relation rival with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.03** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Klara在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Klara用牙咬断，重新来。Katharina Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Klara把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1926.03** `r3-teen.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/expelled` tags: education, boarding_school, dormitory, homesickness, class, reform, medical, schooling
  舍监从Klara的箱底翻出父亲留下的折刀，说寄宿学校不许带凶器。Klara解释那是削葡萄枝用的，没有人听。第二天，她背着行李站在校门口，Alois Lechner 2追出来塞给她一块肥皂。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +expelled_from_boarding_school; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; education none_

- **1926.07** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/fall-on-the-ice` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Elisabeth Bauer抓住Klara的手腕，Berta Hofer跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Klara被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (a fall on the bridge rail); state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 41; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 0; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +yf18_bridge_scare; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1926.10** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sent-to-factory` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Josef Bauer把Klara的书包从钉子上取下来，说厂里招装订工，明天就去。Grete Stein傍晚来家里，站在门口劝了半个钟头，Josef Bauer只说灶上还坐着锅。第二天清早，Klara穿着改短的围裙出门，书包留在门后，里面还有半块橡皮。头一个月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_factory; role; education none_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, rural, agriculture, small_town
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Klara翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Anna Winter在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Klara翻回来时，Josef把一支烟塞进Klara嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 46 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; health -0.030000000000000027 (cold and fear on the bridge); reputation 0.019999999999999997; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Anton Winter 2把一本拉丁文课本推到Klara面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Klara翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Klara把纸抄了一遍，塞进Elisabeth Bauer的历史作业本；那天晚上，Klara第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.09** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/three-on-two-tickets` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Klara最后还是去了，Emil和Elisabeth Bauer已经坐在第三排。Klara在过道边加了一把椅子，三个人挤在两个座位上；引座员来查票，Emil说Klara是自己的妹妹，Klara在黑暗里笑得直抖，笑完才发现Elisabeth Bauer的手一直放在Emil的袖子上。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 56 -> 59; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 23 -> 28; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.010000000000000002; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Ferdinand Moser把Klara叫到巷口，问Emil叫什么、住在哪间屋。Klara答到一半就停住了。Ferdinand Moser说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Klara回到Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg时，Emil正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Klara。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000006; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1928.04** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/caught-by-the-watchman` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josef Weiss的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Josef已经跑没影了，Klara的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Josef Weiss把Klara带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Klara一路上只担心Josef会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +yf18_prater_caught; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office)_

- **1928.04** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, rural, agriculture
  Klara把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Josef在门外笑，说去年还见Klara从木塔上跳下去；Klara没有解释，只隔着板缝看Anna Winter把水撩到肩上。回家时，Klara的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:185; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1928.08** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-letter-changes-hands` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, rural
  Klara把写给Josef的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:184; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1928.12** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/tie-the-sleeve-and-work` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, rural, agriculture
  Klara把袖口卷到肘上，用麻绳扎紧，替k. u. k. Landwehr-Infanterie, Wiener Ergänzungsbezirk把一叠纸送进压印机。前两百张很顺，第二百零一张时，纸角卡住，Klara伸手去拨，机器没有停；等Grete Bauer拉开Klara，食指的指甲已经翻了起来。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (a hand caught in an unguarded press); condition; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 28 -> 20; cash -2 (a doctor and a bandage); flag +yf18_press_injury; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1929.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, rural, agriculture, small_town
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Anna Winter写走了。Klara在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Anna Winter说没关系，把手放回Klara背后；第三支曲子响起时，Josef在门口冲Klara比了个手势，Klara假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Klara和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Klara在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 12; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Anna Winter三步远的地方拐进面包店。Klara买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 51; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 2 -> 5; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/borrowed-specimen-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Rosa Huber说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Klara拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1929.07** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Klara推刨子。Klara推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Klara，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Katharina Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  教授的办公室在二楼拐角，推荐条在桌上摊开。Grete Stein念了一遍条子上的评语，教授用铅笔在课表边上写了“旁听”两个字。走廊里贴着正式生的名单，Klara从头看到尾，没有自己的名字。
  _effects: cash -8 (技术学院旁听学费); education higher; flag +auditing_higher_course; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  考试周里，Klara把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Klara用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 48 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 2_

- **1929.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Josef说该自己走，Klara说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Klara摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: mood -0.06999999999999995; health -0.030000000000000027 (在冰冷的楼梯上坐到半夜)_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Klara上一个东家是谁。Klara说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Klara被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Klara站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07000000000000006; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Alois Hofer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

  一个市民代表团真的来到Klara门口，带来一份任命书：公共礼仪官，负责节庆、吊唁和接待。职位不大，薪水也不高，但上面有印章，有任期，有一间可以锁门的办公室。Klara把任命书读了三遍，才确认这不是戏票。
  _effects: organization_found 市民礼仪处 (state); position 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:004491; reputation 0.08000000000000002; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction

  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Anna Winter。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 51 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 18; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Elisabeth Weiss 2在报上写，这幅画“把贫穷涂成了风景”，还点了Klara的名字。画廊老板把报纸剪下来贴在门口，来看热闹的人多了。Klara站在画前，听见有人笑着说，这就是那个被骂的。
  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; flag +critic_mocked_painting; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; knowledge “the review accused the painting of making poverty picturesqu”_

- **1930.06** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1930.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1930.11** `hs18-paper.pulled-column::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/read-the-blank` tags: newspaper, censorship, reading, daily, rumor, history, reform, medical
  Klara站在报摊前，把那份报纸翻到最后一栏。原先登连载的位置只剩一块空白，边上印着“版面调整”。Maria Huber 2低声说，铅版是夜里被抽走的；排队的人却已经开始猜下一期会不会连名字也换掉。
  _effects: knowledge “报纸最后一栏被抽掉，不是因为版面调整。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +read_pulled_column_

- **1930.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Alois Hofer没有答应，只说现在不合适。Klara把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004490_

- **1931.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Klara下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Elisabeth Huber从字母表教起，Klara把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Klara第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Klara用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill_

  Grete Stein说识字班已经满员，问Klara要不要改上算术课。Klara点头。课上讲的是工钱、尺码和利息，Klara把每一道题都抄在烟盒背面。月底算工钱时，Klara第一次发现工头少记了两个钟头，拿着纸去问，工头把硬币补了回来。算术课学费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_numeracy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002744_

  _effects: organization_found 市民礼仪处 (state); position 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:004982; reputation 0.08000000000000002; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Klara陪Maria Stein去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Maria Stein用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Klara没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。

- **1931.04** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Klara把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Franz Stein放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Helene Winter在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1931.06** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/child-at-the-bear-pit` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, reform
  一个男孩翻过熊池的矮栏去捡帽子。Klara没有喊，怕惊动母熊，只提着装苹果的桶绕到下风处，把桶敲得咚咚响。母熊转身时，孩子被Alois Lechner一把拽了上来；Klara的腿在后来的很多年里还会在梦里发软。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (the bear-pit rescue left the legs shaking); reputation 0.06; relation friend with person-alois-lechner; cash 2 (reward from the child's parents); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:296; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +saved_child_at_zoo; knowledge “母熊被苹果桶引开时，孩子已经挂在{keeper}的胳膊上了。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1931.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Klara把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; skill_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-passed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Helene Kraus从考场出来，手里攥着准考证，说最后一道题做出来了。放榜那天，Klara陪Helene Kraus去看榜，名字在中间偏下，够了。Helene Kraus跳起来，又立刻站好，回头问Klara能不能把旧课本留下。报名费两克朗，Klara替Helene Kraus交了。
  _effects: cash -2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_passed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000177:child_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Lena把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Klara留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Klara在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

- **1932.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Ferdinand Bauer的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Ferdinand Bauer算了一夜，第二天把Klara送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  正式职位没有落到Klara头上，但剧院正在排一出关于权力的戏。Maria Moser说Klara的形象合适，可以在舞台上扮演那位即将被处决的国王。
  _effects: state_add stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:339; reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +stage_king; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  两年过去，Klara已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Klara也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 48; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1932.01** `task-vienna-family-care.courtship-invitation/polite-refusal-church` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Berta Hofer把手里的 Semmel 换到另一只手，说家里不许她单独跟人出门，连星期天下午也不行。她声音不大，但话说得很清楚，随后补一句：教堂下个月有集体郊游，母亲也去，Klara若愿意可以一道走。Klara谢过她，转身时纸袋被捏出一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1932.05** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/understudy-contract` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, reform, medical
  Berta Hofer演主角，Klara拿到的是替角合同：国王的戏服先按主角的尺寸做，Klara只能在排练时穿。导演说这不是坏事，至少不用负责谢幕。首演夜，Klara坐在后台，听见前台每一次掌声都像在敲自己的门。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:340; cash 1 (替角的排练补贴); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +understudy_king; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; flag -stage_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  Klara差四分，没有被录取。图书馆馆长看过Klara的笔记，问愿不愿意来做编目助手，工钱不多，但可以随便看书。Klara第二天就去上工，把书架从A排到Z，手指被纸边割了好几道口子。每月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (借书证背面的铅笔号); flag +library_post; role_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004779_

- **1933.01** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/real-tiny-office` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, reform, medical
  一个市民代表团真的来到Klara门口，带来一份任命书：公共礼仪官，负责节庆、吊唁和接待。职位不大，薪水也不高，但上面有印章，有任期，有一间可以锁门的办公室。Klara把任命书读了三遍，才确认这不是戏票。
  _effects: organization_found 市民礼仪处 (state); position 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:006069; reputation 0.08000000000000002; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1933.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Mitzi抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Mitzi用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Klara把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 18 -> 13; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Klara把用过的课本捆成一摞，提到旧书摊。Maria Stein一本本翻，把缺页的挑出来，最后给了五克朗。Klara接过钱，看见自己写在页边的笔记被Maria Stein随手撕掉，想说什么，还是没说，只把撕下的纸片捡了回来。纸片被油墨粘住，展开了。
  _effects: cash 5 (卖掉的课本); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.06** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Klara的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Klara家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005872_

  Josef Bauer问起工钱和房租，Klara报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Elisabeth Huber低着头把面包掰成小块，Josef Bauer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000006; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1933.10** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Klara把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Anton Moser站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Klara手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000323:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

  Klara用夜校的结业证报了市政招考，考的是公文抄写和简单算术。考场设在旧修道院，桌腿一高一低，Klara垫了张折起来的传单。放榜那天，Alois Lechner比Klara先看到名字，隔着街喊了一声，声音哑了。月薪六克朗，Klara第一次有了自己的办公桌。
  _effects: cash 6 (借书证背面的铅笔号); flag +passed_self_taught_exam; role; relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:428; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Klara把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Klara来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 4 -> 5; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.03** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +rent_installment_plan_

  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1934.07** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  屋里只有一张床，Klara把椅子拼在床边守了三夜，给Katharina Bauer敷药换布。请假扣的工钱让人心疼，但腿伤见好；第四天早上，Katharina Bauer自己端起了粥碗。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱); health 0.06999999999999962 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  Klara没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Klara去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1936.09** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Luise Huber在门口堵了三天，第四天把Klara的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Klara把办公室的钥匙交给Mitzi Bauer 2，没有带走任何文件。

- **1937.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/mistaken-identity` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, crisis
  一个代表团走错了房间，把Klara当成新来的部长，围着说了十分钟的请求。Klara没有纠正。等人走后，Klara坐在那张不属于自己的椅子上，听见走廊里有人在找真正的部长。

- **1937.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Klara看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999925; health -0.03999999999999998 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1937.05** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/caught-in-darkroom` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  显影液被Mitzi Bauer 2碰翻，整盆药水泼在一张婚礼合影上。新郎的脸变成一团灰雾，店主抓住Klara的领子；Anton来后，两人被关了一夜。第二天，Klara交了罚款三克朗，Mitzi Bauer 2被新娘的家人追着要赔照片；那顶帽子也留在了拘留室。
  _effects: cash -3 (破坏暗房的罚款（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.040000000000000036; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:533; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; condition; flag +destroyed_a_wedding_portrait_

- **1937.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara和Josef把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Josef守前半夜，Klara守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Klara的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josef Bauer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: flag +night_care_duty_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Klara没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。

  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.020000000000000018 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1942.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

- **1943.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Grete Moser在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Klara。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Klara没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: reputation -0.050000000000000044; mood -0.06000000000000005; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:752; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; flag +office_under_siege_

  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012292_

  会议开到最后，只通过成立一个研究委员会，说三个月后交报告。散会时Klara和Emil Weiss 2在门口商量下一步，有人提议先发动街区签名，把工价和房租的数字附在报告后面。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  工头第二次出来时眼皮都没抬。Rosa Winter把工具往Klara脚边一放，说去去就回，回来时用围裙兜着面包，汤罐用旧布裹着，两人在墙根分着吃完。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002100:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  报纸把Klara称作“新任主席”，照片很大，办公室很漂亮。真正的决定在另一间屋里做出。Klara每天签字，签的都是别人写好的东西。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; reputation 0.010000000000000009; position 名誉主席 @org-sdap; flag +puppet_office; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

  鹦鹉死在第二个冬天。窗缝里灌进来的风把笼布吹开，它缩在食盆旁边，像一团被揉皱的灰纸。Franz Bauer 3说条件没有完成，十二克朗归了姑妈的教子；Klara把鸟埋在院子里，留下那只笼子。春天，笼子里长出一丛野草，Klara没有拔。
  _effects: cash -2 (鹦鹉的药费和笼布（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation kin with person-ferdinand-winter; condition; flag +parrot_died_before_the_will; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1944.08** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  玛丽亚在市场拱门下拦住Klara，声音不大但周围人都听得见：三个克朗。Klara翻遍口袋只摸出几个十字币。玛丽亚把布袋从Klara胳膊上解下来，转身走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.15000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000177:child_

- **1944.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Mitzi Lechner的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Mitzi Lechner提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Klara躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Klara去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Katharina Kral在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Friedrich Berg没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.05999999999999994; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:013655; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

  仓库里一股潮味。Klara蹲下把工具箱打开，凿子用油布包着，刨子柄上缠着哥哥自己的麻绳。老板说人搬走两个月了。Klara把工具卖给旧货商，三克朗，买票时数了两遍。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉哥哥留下的工具换路费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1945.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Anton把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Klara打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

  Klara把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Klara自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 5 (编辑部的前厅); flag +manuscript_published_with_cuts; role; knowledge “the editor cut the political passage and kept the rest”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗)_

- **1946.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Klara嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Klara用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:014374; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:897; cash 2 (night taxi fares); flag +taxi_night_shift; knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  Klara没传话，只在二楼门口站了一会儿，提醒Maria Lechner夜里把门闩好，说楼梯间有人爱嚼舌头。Maria Lechner点点头，把门闩推过去，发出很轻的一声。此后送活的人还是照来，闲话却渐渐没人接。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002384:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  旧锅底还沾着昨天的面糊，Klara拿它盛炭时有点不好意思。Anton没说什么，只把火钳递过来，让她自己夹。炭在锅里红得发暗，一路端回屋没灭。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Klara和Rosa Moser 2合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。

  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  办事处后门的Alois Weiss 3把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Alois Weiss 3没有再出现。

  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”_

  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Klara让Franz Kral记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Franz Kral只说了一句，明早再核一遍。

- **1957.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Klara在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Klara拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Rosa Lechner找到Klara时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007657:child_

- **1957.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Elisabeth Winter追出来问怎么不领了，Klara说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Klara把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; food_

- **1914.05** `vienna-infant-household.cradle-fall/caught-by-sister` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Mitzi Lechner的石板笔滚到墙角，Mitzi已经滑到摇床边沿。Mitzi Lechner的手正好垫在下面，Alois Lechner赶来时只看见两个孩子一起坐在地上。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (从摇床边缘滑落，被垫子接住); relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1916.07** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/doctor-bill` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Mitzi烧得嘴唇发干，Alois Lechner把湿布敷上去，凉了再换，撑到窗外发白才去请医生。医生把听诊器贴在Mitzi背上，留下三包药粉，收了一古尔登。Alois Lechner从围裙内袋里摸出硬币，一枚一枚排在桌上，差一点不够。
  _effects: cash -1 (医生的诊金（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (服药后退烧); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1917.07** `p9-childhood-household.supper-bowl/gave-potato-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  锅里的汤只剩一层，Mitzi用勺子在锅底捞了捞，把最大的一块土豆舀进Emil Lechner碗里。Emil Lechner低头就吃，Rosa Lechner把自己的香肠片撕开，一半放进Mitzi碗底，一半留在盘边。Mitzi吃到碗底才发现，耳朵有点热。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000209:child; mood 0.03_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Mitzi空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Alois Lechner来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Mitzi用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Mitzi把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Alois Lechner。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Mitzi前面的大婶一直往后靠，Mitzi就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Mitzi的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000004 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

- **1919.07** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Mitzi的头一直裹到脚，Alois Lechner抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Alois Lechner把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Mitzi躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Alois Lechner的手一直按着被角。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Gustav Moser把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Mitzi伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Gustav Moser从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Mitzi手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Mitzi一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1920.03** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Mitzi靠在墙边等Alois Lechner，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Mitzi开始发烧，Alois Lechner把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Mitzi的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Alois Lechner的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  掌柜不肯赊，Anna Moser从队尾挤过来，说这户人家的男人在印刷所做了十年，欠不了一筐煤。掌柜把“两赫勒”写在门牌号旁边，煤只装了小半筐。Mitzi抱着筐经过Anna Moser身边，Anna Moser替Mitzi把绳结紧了紧。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1921.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Mitzi在第三圈超过Karl Kraus，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Mitzi口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Karl Kraus从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Mitzi站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Anna Lechner睡醒哭了一声，Mitzi把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Alois Lechner回来时，Mitzi正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000595:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1922.07** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Mitzi弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Mitzi把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Alois Lechner把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Mitzi每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Otto Winter家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Mitzi递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Otto Winter端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Mitzi把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1923.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Mitzi把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Franz Lechner走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Franz Lechner合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

  Mitzi把石板放在膝盖上，Elisabeth Huber用粉笔在木板上写字母。教室里只有一排课桌，后排的孩子坐在倒扣的奶桶上。下课铃是一只旧牛铃，Elisabeth Huber摇的时候，窗外的羊也跟着叫。

  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +village_school_reopened_under_district; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; education primary_

  Josef Lechner从铁栅栏后面招手，让Mitzi从厨房旁的侧门进去。花园里有钢琴、一只吃饱的猫和一盘没动过的奶油蛋糕。Anton Winter盯着Mitzi的鞋，Josef Lechner却把最大的那块推到Mitzi面前，说吃不完也是喂猫。那天回家，Mitzi的鞋底沾着花园里的湿土，舍不得刮掉，像把整条巷子关在门外。

  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/relative-introduces-trial-half-day` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  亲戚把他带到工头面前，工头让他下星期来试半天，先看裁纸和搬版。母亲熨平的衬衫领子还是硬得硌脖子，他站在厂门口把姓名说了两遍。
  _effects: flag +half_day_trial_at_big_printshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.teacher-recommendation/night-school-first` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:education
  “先念夜校。”Elisabeth Huber用铅笔敲了敲练习本的封面，说簿记和德语拿到证书，Elisabeth Huber才肯写推荐。Mitzi问清一学期要一克朗，又问了上课的钟点，回家路上绕到六区，隔着玻璃看教室里的黑板。第二天Mitzi把铜币数好，报名时还问了几句考试怎么算。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; cash -1 (夜校一学期报名费和课本钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +enrolled_night_school; skill_

- **1925.06** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Lechner看了看Mitzi的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Mitzi回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +apprenticeship_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1926.01** `kernel-work.task-apprenticeship-place::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::work-apprentice-reform-office/work-apprentice-subsidized` tags: task, work, apprenticeship, training, placement, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner翻过学校成绩单，把Mitzi的名字写进市立学徒名册：介绍费由市政补贴，师傅每月多领两克朗带徒津贴。Franz Winter原来嫌麻烦，看到津贴才点头。合同仍是三年，头一年月钱十四克朗；Mitzi把盖章的通知拿回家，母亲把它压在镜子下面。
  _effects: employment Lehrling @ org-winter-print; education apprenticeship; role; cash 8 (市政学徒安置补贴); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; flag +municipal_apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1926.02** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Mitzi趁Alois Lechner去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Mitzi趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Mitzi说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:129; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.02** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, rural, agriculture, small_town
  地理课上，Mitzi在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Mitzi认出是Rosa的笔迹，趁Friedrich Kral转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Mitzi把地图册塞进Rosa的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:130; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_atlas_note; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.03** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/offer-declined-black-humour` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, rural, agriculture, small_town, peace, vineyard
  Mitzi把入学通知折成纸船放进河里；家里少了一张嘴吃饭，也少了一个读书人。
  _effects: cash 10 (sold the textbooks instead); education none_

- **1926.11** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, rural, agriculture
  下一站，Mitzi追下车，把那只手套递还给Rosa。Rosa说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Mitzi回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:131; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi在衣帽间堵住Elisabeth Bauer，问那两张票怎么到了Josef Lechner手里。Elisabeth Bauer说Mitzi从来没敢开口，自己只是替Mitzi把话说了；Mitzi把票根揉成一团，塞进Elisabeth Bauer的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 20; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000079:child; reputation -0.02; mood -0.049999999999999996_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:130; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Rosa。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 44 -> 49; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 17 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.vaccination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.vaccination-reform/reform-class-vaccinated` tags: health, vaccination, school, parent, public_health, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Winter 2让全班按座位排队，Otto Winter在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Mitzi时，Anton Winter 2替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (种痘室门口的袖子); condition; flag +vaccinated_in_school_campaign; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1927.07** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-factory` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Alois Lechner把Mitzi的书包从钉子上取下来，说厂里招装订工，明天就去。Grete Stein傍晚来家里，站在门口劝了半个钟头，Alois Lechner只说灶上还坐着锅。第二天清早，Mitzi穿着改短的围裙出门，书包留在门后，里面还有半块橡皮。头一个月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_factory; role; education none_

- **1927.08** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/leave-before-the-last-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi在最后一支曲子前溜出礼堂，沿着教堂外墙走了一圈。窗子里传来手风琴声，Mitzi数到第三遍副歌才往家走；家里问舞会好不好，Mitzi说人太多，脚疼。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 44; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 17; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1927.12** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/forge-the-permit` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi在市政厅的表格上替Alois Lechner签了名，又把日期往前挪了三天。办事员看了Mitzi一眼，问父亲怎么没来；Mitzi说他病在床上。印章落下去时，Mitzi的手心全是汗，出门才发现自己把回执捏成了两半。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:158; cash -2 (stamps and a clerk's fee); flag +yf18_forged_permit; reputation -0.01; mood 0.009999999999999995_

  工会的人在厂门口拦住Mitzi，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Mitzi每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1928.01** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/go-alone` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把车票放在桌上，说家里给她找了活，走不了。Mitzi把两张票叠在一起，撕掉一张，另一张塞进内袋；Rosa问Mitzi一个人怕不怕，Mitzi说不怕，出门时却把地址写给了她。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 65 -> 75; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 20 -> 25; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_go_alone; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1928.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Mitzi把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Mitzi没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 25 -> 33; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 75 -> 73_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003551_

  Mitzi把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Mitzi在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 65 -> 77; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 33 -> 35; mood 0.010000000000000002_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick

  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.leave-bad-workshop/walked-off-unpaid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Mitzi以为走就走了，工钱总会结。等到月底，家里人把空钱袋翻过来，Mitzi才回到作坊。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne正在给新学徒示范上墨，头也没抬，说人没打招呼就走，只能给几个铜币。Mitzi站在门口，听见里面的机器又转起来，铜币在掌心硌得生疼。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (没拿到最后一个月的整月工钱（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07999999999999999; unemployment (不告而别离开作坊); relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1928.04** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-letter-changes-hands` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, reform
  Mitzi把写给Lena的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:187; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.05; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

  学校没有多余名额，只给Mitzi留了夜校的位置。白天在工厂站一天，晚上再去教室坐三个钟头；老师说先试试，撑不住也不算丢人。
  _effects: state_add night-school state:world-vienna-1912:188_

  Mitzi和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Mitzi在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 12; mood 0.02_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:night-school/annual/held` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick

  _effects: state_parameter night-school.progress 0 -> 15_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.dropout-return::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/returned-to-school` tags: education, dropout, return, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi退学半年后，在工厂门口看见以前的同学背着书包走过。Mitzi回家翻出课本，书页已经发黄。第二天，Mitzi去找Elisabeth Huber，Elisabeth Huber让Mitzi先跟班旁听两周。Mitzi坐在最后一排，把落下的笔记一页页补回来。两个月后，Mitzi的座位从最后一排换到中间。
  _effects: flag +returned_to_school; education secondary_

- **1929.03** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/jump-from-the-tower` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Mitzi爬上河边的木跳台，下面的水黑得像一块铁。Hedwig在岸上抬头看，Mitzi想挥手，脚下一滑就掉了下去；浮上来时，嘴里全是河水的腥味，岸上的人都在鼓掌，Mitzi却只听见自己的心跳。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:221; reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_jumped_tower; mood 0.05_

  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:220; flag +yf18_burned_fingers; mood 0.009999999999999995; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.01; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000271:child; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away-for-accent` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi刚开口问价，Helene Winter就听出口音，说这栋楼只租给本区人。Mitzi说自己在附近上工，Helene Winter把招租纸重新贴回门上，说下个月再来问。Mitzi沿着街口走了很久，才找到另一张招租纸。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +lodging_refused_for_accent; knowledge “some buildings rent only to families from the district”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1930.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Luise Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1930.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Josefine Hofer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Josefine Hofer敲开Mitzi的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.05_

- **1930.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Alois Lechner问起工钱和房租，Mitzi报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Maria Weiss低着头把面包掰成小块，Alois Lechner往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1930.12** `ad20-favor-called-in::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/do-the-favor` tags: favor, obligation, everyday, trust, debt, risk, reform, medical
  Katharina Bauer说只需要Mitzi在明天下午去一趟车站，把一个箱子交给穿灰外套的人。Mitzi问箱子里是什么；Katharina Bauer说“你不需要知道”。Mitzi还是去了，因为当年Katharina Bauer替Mitzi垫过一笔医药费。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:260; cash -1 (the trip to the station); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; flag +favor_done; mood -0.009999999999999998; knowledge “箱子里是什么，邻居没有说。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 18 -> 24; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 52 -> 49; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state

- **1931.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Hedwig三步远的地方拐进面包店。Mitzi买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 71; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 5 -> 8; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/kept` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  那笔私下的安排又过了一年。没有人提起，但Mitzi在街上遇见对方时，两个人都多停了一拍。Mitzi知道，沉默本身也是利息。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 55 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 55 -> 53; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 20 -> 22; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Winter把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Mitzi去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Alois Lechner给Mitzi一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Mitzi把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1931.03** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  教堂后院的铁锅冒着白汽，排队的人把各种罐子捧在胸前。Otto Winter 2穿着灰围裙一勺一勺地分，轮到Mitzi时锅底已经露出来，勺子刮出响声，还是刮了半勺倒进罐里。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1931.04** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1931.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Mitzi胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中0.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.030000000000000006 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  Mitzi捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Otto Lechner点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1932.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, reform, medical
  Mitzi陪Grete Stein去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Grete Stein用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Mitzi没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-grete-stein_

- **1932.04** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.059999999999999984; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1932.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Mitzi把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1932.10** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-name-taken` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  玻璃后面的招工纸被门房用尺子压平，Mitzi的名字写在横线本上，墨迹歪着。旁边的Gustav Berg 2凑过来看了一眼，说这厂子去年也招过两回，录用的多半是厂里老人的亲戚。
  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1932.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Mitzi把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Maria Weiss把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood -0.09; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1933.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, reform
  Emil Weiss 2坐在床边，Mitzi用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Emil Weiss 2闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 49 -> 54; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 30 -> 27; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +hairwash_sunday_

  Mitzi和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Mitzi在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 54 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 27 -> 24; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `r13-civ.care-fever::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/convulsion-and-doctor` tags: health, fever, care, child, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Lechner忽然在枕头上抽起来，牙关咬得咯咯响。Mitzi抱着孩子冲下楼，敲开Friedrich Weiss的门，鞋都没顾上换。Friedrich Weiss掐着人中，让把孩子放进温水里，又收走了四克朗的出诊费。
  _effects: cash -4 (湿布换到第三盆水); condition; flag +child_fever_convulsion; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003101:child; knowledge “a fever convulsion needs a lukewarm bath, not cold water”_

- **1933.03** `hs18-revolution.leaflet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/deliver-to-the-printer` tags: revolution, leaflet, cinema, neighbor, politics, history, reform, medical
  Mitzi按传单上的暗号找到印刷所。印刷所的人正在洗版，手上全是墨，说今晚还要再印五百张。Mitzi帮忙把纸捆上自行车，换回一句“别走正街”。回家时，Mitzi绕了三座桥，车链子响得像打鼓。
  _effects: cash 1 (a few coins for carrying the paper bundles); skill; flag +delivered_revolution_leaflets_

- **1933.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Mitzi手里。Friedrich Weiss的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Maria Huber隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Friedrich Weiss再见到Mitzi时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Mitzi站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.07999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1934.01** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/neighbor-help-night` tags: health, family, home
  邻居听见动静，端来热水和药片，替Mitzi守了半宿。天亮Maria Lechner退了烧，Mitzi把半块面包送过去，对方推了两下，还是收下了。
  _effects: health 0.08 (邻居帮助后烧退); food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.invite-cafe::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::cafe-war/ration-refused` tags: task, social, cafe, friendship, invitation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Huber站在门口没有进来，说自家的糖票也已经见底。Mitzi把壶从炉上端下来，倒进两个杯子，硬塞给Maria Huber一杯。Maria Huber喝完，把杯子在围裙上擦干，说明天轮到自己家去排队。
  _effects: food; flag +shared_last_sugar; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1934.05** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sign-here` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Gustav Berg 2把借据铺在桌上，先让Mitzi看利息，又让Mitzi看还款日期。Mitzi用指头一行行划过去，在最后一行停住：若逾期，担保人承担。Gustav Berg 2说只是走个形式；Mitzi还是把担保人的名字问清楚，才签了字。
  _effects: cash 6 (a loan was signed with a private creditor); state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:430; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; flag +loan_signed; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “借据上写着若逾期，担保人承担。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1934.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Mitzi把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1934.11** `major-education-university-registration/provisional-matriculation` tags: education, university, migration, major
  Otto Winter 2收下材料，在表格边上写了“临时”两个字：先给一学期学籍，圣诞节前通过拉丁文补考，否则注册作废，学费不退。Mitzi交了三十克朗，把临时学籍证明放进内袋。那天下午，Mitzi在利奥波德城找到一间合租房，窗下就是电车轨道。
  _effects: education higher; cash -30 (provisional registration fee); role; flag +provisional_matriculation_

- **1935.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Mitzi对Maria Kral 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Maria Kral 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Mitzi的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 30 -> 36; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 43 -> 40; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/kept` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  那笔私下的安排又过了一年。没有人提起，但Mitzi在街上遇见对方时，两个人都多停了一拍。Mitzi知道，沉默本身也是利息。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 50 -> 54; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 60 -> 58; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 15 -> 17; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `r13-fam.set-up-household::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/move-in-with-in-laws` tags: task, family, household, housing, marriage, vienna, urban, crisis
  屋子没租成，Otto Stein的母亲腾出半间前屋，条件是新婚夫妇每月交饭钱。Mitzi把箱子搬进去时，Elisabeth Stein站在楼下看，嘴角带着笑。当天晚上，两代人在一张桌上吃饭，谁的筷子都没有先动。
  _effects: cash -3 (第一间自己的屋子（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +moved_in_with_in_laws; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; knowledge “the couple pays board to the partner’s mother”; household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Mitzi被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Mitzi低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 36 -> 46; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007539_

- **1935.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Alois Lechner提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Otto Stein把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Otto Stein说不是Mitzi的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Mitzi心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1935.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Mitzi在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.11999999999999998 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1935.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.03; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Luise Stein。接生的人收六克朗，Otto Stein在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Mitzi起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:496_

- **1936.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Lena坐在床边，Mitzi用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Lena闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 58 -> 63; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 46 -> 43; mood 0.04000000000000001_

  Mitzi和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Mitzi在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 63 -> 67; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 43 -> 40; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  Mitzi把一整年的进货、赊账和折旧做成三栏账，Elisabeth Huber看完只挑出一处小数点点错。结业考试那天，Mitzi用算盘打了七遍，余额终于对上。证书发下来，Mitzi把它夹在账本第一页，每次翻账都先看见自己的名字。纸角压得很平。
  _effects: flag +bookkeeping_certificate; skill_

- **1936.02** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Katharina Berg，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Katharina Berg说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1936.09** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Otto Stein把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Mitzi留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Mitzi在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999997; role_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.06; skill; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; flag +quiet_surplus; knowledge “审计报告里只有一句“未发现异常”，但窗口提前开门被办事的人记住了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.03_

- **1939.03** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
