  车间里管互助会的Grete Huber 2在午休时把本子摊在Mitzi的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Mitzi从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Grete Huber 2当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1945.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Maria Stein没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Mitzi躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Mitzi去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1946.01** `r14-fam.begin-affair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/key-accepted` tags: task, family, marriage, forbidden_affair, betrayal, vienna, urban, postwar
  Alois Weiss把一把小钥匙放在咖啡杯旁边，说旅馆后门的房间按周租，不会有人问名字。Mitzi把钥匙收进内袋，指尖碰到里面的车票和家门钥匙。两人没有多坐，出门时一前一后，Mitzi在街角回头，看见Alois Weiss还站在灯下。
  _effects: cash -3 (旅馆后门的第二把钥匙); mood 0.06; condition; flag +affair_started; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1946.03** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Maria Lechner 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Mitzi把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Maria Lechner 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1946.11** `ad20-newspaper-interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/printed-as-a-person` tags: media, interview, newspaper, everyday, public, reputation, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Stein 2把笔记本合上，问Mitzi能不能再讲一件小事，不要那些大词。Mitzi说去年冬天在车站帮一个陌生人看过箱子；Elisabeth Stein 2记下箱子的大小和颜色。第二天报纸出来，Mitzi的名字在第三版，旁边是一张没有拍好的侧脸。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:901; reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +interview_printed_kindly; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “报纸第三版登了采访，旁边是一张没有拍好的侧脸。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1946.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Mitzi在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Mitzi去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Mitzi数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

  Elisabeth Moser洗外套时从口袋里摸出两张衣帽间的号牌，号码不是同一晚的。Mitzi回家时，桌上摆着那两张硬纸片，Alois Weiss的围巾搭在椅背上。Elisabeth Moser没有提高声音，只问要不要把箱子先收好；Mitzi看见窗外的雪落在消防梯上，一直没有化。
  _effects: cash -3 (衣帽间的号牌（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.18; reputation -0.1; flag +affair_discovered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014769_

  Mitzi在门口卖火柴，一盒顶平时三盒的价。买的人不少，有人划亮一根照路，又舍不得扔掉。Emil Kraus从柜台后喊“别在店里点火”；Mitzi把剩下的火柴塞进包里，觉得这钱赚得比面包还快。

  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 60 -> 52; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 55 -> 52; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 40 -> 38; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

  明信片正面是卡尔教堂，背面只写了三行：天气很好，梨树开花了，勿念。Anna Winter 2把卡片举到灯下，说梨树四月才开花，现在才三月。Mitzi认得那是Helene Kral的笔迹，也认得“梨树”是他们约好的词。Mitzi把明信片收进内袋，说大概写错了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +understood_postcard_cipher; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; knowledge “the pear blossom on the postcard means the meeting is moved”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  Alois Lechner问起工钱和房租，Mitzi报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Mitzi Bauer低着头把面包掰成小块，Alois Lechner往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。

  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); mood -0.05999999999999994_

  Grete Moser进门后没有先翻账本，而是问Mitzi是不是在战前服过役。两个人对上了部队编号，Grete Moser把帽子放在桌上，说利息可以按小额储蓄申报，罚款就免了。Berta Hofer 2松了一口气，手里的计算尺却还停在半空，像是不知道该不该落下去。

- **1951.01** `kernel-culture.radio-broadcast::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university::civic-radio-reform/civic-radio-reform-dispute` tags: culture, radio, broadcast, writing, reform, vienna, urban, postwar
  技术员说线路只能通到三个教室，Franz Kral还是让她播。她读到一半，发现自己在替学校念招生通知；关掉话筒后，她问Franz Kral“这也算文化吗”，Franz Kral没有回答。她关掉话筒，听见走廊里学生在模仿她的声音。
  _effects: flag +school_broadcast_dispute; reputation -0.009999999999999995; knowledge “学校线路只能通三个教室，广播内容常被改成招生通知”; cash 1 (学校广播的短稿费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1951.06** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/sign-here` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Helene Bauer把借据铺在桌上，先让Mitzi看利息，又让Mitzi看还款日期。Mitzi用指头一行行划过去，在最后一行停住：若逾期，担保人承担。Helene Bauer说只是走个形式；Mitzi还是把担保人的名字问清楚，才签了字。
  _effects: cash 6 (a loan was signed with a private creditor); state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:1116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “借据上写着若逾期，担保人承担。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +private_bargain_active_

  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -6 (石碑上的第两百零七个名字); flag +paid_to_correct_memorial_name; relation colleague with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; knowledge “two names on the memorial are misspelled”_

  Mitzi在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Berta Huber 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Mitzi把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。

  捐款交出去三个月，诊所还没有开门，先来了一辆新的弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。Mitzi Bauer 2把账本摊在桌上，说委员会认为“体面的门面”也是诊所的一部分。Rosa Winter在门口骂了一句，Mitzi把账本翻到最后一页，发现自己捐的钱只剩一半。

  烟从阁楼窗口往外翻，Mitzi用湿毛巾捂住口鼻，摸到楼梯口。Katharina Berg瘫在门边，鞋底已经烧软；Mitzi把人拖到院子里，自己的手掌粘掉一层皮。消防队来后，把烧黑的楼梯封了，谁也不能再上去。邻居的鞋底在门槛上留下两块黑印。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (burnt palms and smoke inhalation during the rescue); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; role; cash -2 (bandages, ointment and a new shirt); knowledge “阁楼起火时烟比火先到，湿毛巾只能顶一会儿；把人拖出来以后手掌会粘掉一层皮。”_

- **1956.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.abandon-project/project-sold` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  房租催到门口，Mitzi把已写的章节卖给一家小报，换了八克朗，够付一个月房租和两顿热饭。长篇锁进抽屉，钥匙搁在盐罐后面，Franz Kral问起时他只说“先不提了”，第二天照常去上工。
  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +novel_shelved_

- **1956.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Friedrich Lechner没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002719:child_

- **1956.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi把通知念给Helene Kral听，Helene Kral摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Mitzi去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food_

- **1956.08** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Grete Lechner说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Mitzi站在原地看着Grete Lechner拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007062:child_

- **1956.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Mitzi看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: health -0.04 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1957.04** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Gustav Stein量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-172_

- **1957.11** `ad20-police-stop::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sent-on` tags: police, legal, street, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Emil Kraus把Mitzi的证件翻了两遍，问这么晚去哪。Mitzi说回家；Emil Kraus看了看地址，又看了看Mitzi的鞋，把证件还回来，说“走大路”。Mitzi走出十几步，才发现自己一直屏着呼吸。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +police_let_go; knowledge “警察看了地址，让走大路。”_

### 282 subject events

- **1917.12** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  二楼窗口有人喊了一声，Josef抬头应着，脚却没动。铁环卡在Mitzi Lechner的鞋边，Mitzi Lechner说要再玩一圈。Josef把铁钩递过去，说明天借你。Mitzi Lechner这才挪开脚，用鞋尖把铁环拨到Josef面前。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Anna Moser家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Josef递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Anna Moser端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Josef把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1918.04** `iw18-school.bell-rope::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-rings-it` tags: childhood, school, bell, comic, play, injury, rural, agriculture
  Grete Stein没有让孩子再爬煤箱，而是把Josef抱起来，让孩子自己抓住铃绳。铃响时，Josef离地很高，能看见院子里所有孩子的头顶。Grete Stein放下孩子后，说“以后想摇铃就来找我”，Josef点头，手心还留着粗麻绳的印子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood 0.04; flag +lifted_to_the_bell_

- **1918.05** `iw18-school.ink-pot::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-blame-sticks` tags: childhood, school, cruelty, shame, teacher, ink, rural, agriculture
  墨水瓶从桌角翻下去，蓝黑的一摊漫过Josef的抄写本。Emil Weiss 2从讲台后抬起头，先看见Josef手上的墨，便让Josef站到墙角。真正碰倒瓶子的Elisabeth Bauer低着头，把脚尖缩进凳子下面，一句话也没有说。
  _effects: mood -0.06; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +ink_pot_blamed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  湿煤灰写出第一笔时，Josef还挺高兴，写到第三行，手一蹭就全花了。Josef Bauer看见了，把自己的旧手帕撕下一角，蘸水给木板擦了底。第二天在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg门口，同学指着那块发黑的木板问是什么，Josef说是石板，对方笑得弯下腰。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.07** `kernel-child.first-school-day::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-school-enrolled` tags: childhood, school, enrolment, teacher, fees, rural, agriculture, small_town
  开学那天，Josef Bauer用旧布给Josef缝了一个书包，里面是一块石板、一支石笔和半块面包。Elisabeth Huber在门口点名，念到Josef时抬头看了一眼，说进来吧，坐第三排。Josef把石板放正，手心全是汗。
  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (slate, chalk and a school bag); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; role; skill; flag +started_school; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Josef把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Otto Stein挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Otto Stein转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Josef站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1919.12** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-chair-is-for-the-dead` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Josef把盘子摆好，说这把椅子是留给去年埋掉的那个人的。Josef Bauer手里的火柴停了一下，屋里只剩煤在炉膛里塌下去的声响。Josef又说，朋友不喜欢哭，所以今天谁都不许哭；说完自己先红了眼睛。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:35; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +friend_named_for_the_dead; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1920.01** `vienna-life-school-day/hungry-in-class` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Josef早上只喝了一碗清汤，第二节课时眼前发黑。Emil Weiss 3让孩子去走廊坐一会儿，给了半块面包；Josef把面包屑拢在手心吃完，胃里还是空的，但看Emil Weiss 3的眼神软了些，回到座位时把腰挺直了一点。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (went to school hungry); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1920.01** `iw18-friend.real-friend::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-friend-gets-jealous` tags: childhood, friendship, imaginary_friend, play, change, rural, agriculture, small_town
  球滚进阴沟，Elisabeth Bauer说是Josef没接住，Josef却转头对空椅子说“都怪你”。Elisabeth Bauer莫名其妙，抱着球走了。Josef一个人站在院子里，忽然觉得那把椅子离自己很远，像别人家窗口里的一件旧家具。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 65 -> 70; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 60 -> 66; mood -0.019999999999999997_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Josef还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Josef知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Josef把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 70 -> 73; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 66 -> 68; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1920.09** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  Josef拼到一半停住，Grete Stein的尺子落在桌角上。放学后楼梯间只剩Josef一个人，把“Notwendigkeit”抄了二十遍，墨水瓶见了底。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1920.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Josef跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Josef从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Emil Weiss喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Josef手心。Josef低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Emil Weiss扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1921.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Josef还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Josef知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Josef把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 73 -> 76; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 68 -> 70; mood 0.02_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  “老师，我能留下来吗？”Josef问完就盯着自己的鞋尖。Emil Weiss 3想了一会儿，说早上来，人少。天没亮，Josef已经站在校门口，手指冻得伸不直。教室里只有炉火和粉笔灰的味道，Emil Weiss 3把一道竖式拆成三行，让Josef跟着念一遍。
  _effects: skill; health -0.020000000000000018 (天没亮出门，早春的冷); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1921.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Elisabeth Huber从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Josef答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Josef回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Elisabeth Huber看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1922.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Josef的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:35; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Josef的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Josef在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.029999999999999995; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Anna Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Josef伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Anna Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Josef手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Josef一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1923.07** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef正低头看卡上的皇帝胡子，一个穿背带裤的男孩从侧面撞过来。卡飞出去，落在马车辙里。Josef扑过去捡，手掌被碎石划开一道口子。Hedwig追到巷子尽头，只抓下一块袖布。回来时Josef还蹲在地上，把沾泥的卡往袖子上擦。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1923.08** `vienna-school-childhood.coal-sled/coal-sled.trade` tags: school, social, childhood
  Gustav Lechner从口袋里摸出一块焦炭，Josef把雪橇推过去。三次滑行之后，焦炭进了自家的炉子，Josef Bauer没问从哪来的。
  _effects: food; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000271:child; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1924.04** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1924.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1924.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Josef在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Josef用牙咬断，重新来。Katharina Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Josef把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1924.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1924.11** `p10-edu-thresholds.buergerschule-exam/exam-waitlist` tags: adolescence, education, school, exam, apprenticeship, work, transition, buergerschule
  走廊尽头的布告贴出名次，二十九号被红笔圈在候补里。Josef Bauer踮脚看了两遍才认出来，问监考的Friedrich Kral是不是还有机会。老师说，九月开学头一天来问，有人不来就有位子。
  _effects: mood -0.14999999999999997; knowledge “商业学校只录二十八人，九月或许有空位补入”; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Mitzi Weiss就从楼梯口上来，盯着Josef看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Mitzi Weiss当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Elisabeth Bauer的雇主。Josef站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Elisabeth Bauer问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1925.06** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Winter看了看Josef的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Josef回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +apprenticeship_refused_

- **1925.11** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Katharina Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1925.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Josef把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1925.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Josef追下车，把那只手套递还给Mitzi。Mitzi说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Josef回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:99; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Josef的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Elisabeth Bauer骂了一句，Berta Hofer却把围巾解下来递给Josef；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 20; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Emil Kraus把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Josef把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Josef只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Emil Kraus在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000726:child; condition_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002731_

  补考那天，Josef在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Grete Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Josef去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education apprenticeship_

  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Josef留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。

  Elisabeth Bauer在第二支曲子前把Klara请走，说Josef答应过要替她看管外套。Josef抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Josef把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。

- **1928.06** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Josef推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Josef Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Josef在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Josef Bauer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1928.07** `p9-childhood-adolescence.secret-in-the-stairwell/secret-ask-worker` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Josef去找Berta Huber，问能不能让Emil留在纸料间。Berta Huber搓着手上洗不掉的墨，说要照规矩通知教区，否则厂里担不起。Josef回来的路上一直看自己的鞋尖。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1928.08** `r2-leisure.maid-follower::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/refused-the-follower` tags: domestic_service, courtship, servant, followers, risk, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josef把门闩推回去，说雨停了再来。Alois Lechner 2在门外站了一会儿，把一封信从门缝底下塞进来，信封上沾着煤灰。Josef没有捡，第二天早上信封被扫进炉子，纸灰从灶口飘出来。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +refused_follower; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; knowledge “the soldier left a letter under the cellar door”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1928.11** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/read-aloud-in-class` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, rural
  Grete Stein在课堂上翻到那封信，没有念出名字，只把句子读了一遍。教室里先是安静，随后有人笑。Emil低下头，说那不是自己写的；Josef看着课本上的拉丁文，一个字也认不出来。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:183; reputation -0.06; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.07000000000000006; flag +letter_read_aloud; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1928.11** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1928.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1929.01** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, rural, agriculture, small_town
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Josef把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josef Bauer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:217; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Josef把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Josef没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 60 -> 58_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Josef，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Josef没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 45 -> 49; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:99; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Josef解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Friedrich Kral也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Josef自己晾。Josef把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Friedrich Kral说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Friedrich Kral哼了一声，算是记下了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.040000000000000036; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1929.02** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/caught-by-the-watchman` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Maria Huber 2的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Anna Winter已经跑没影了，Josef的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Maria Huber 2把Josef带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Josef一路上只担心Anna Winter会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05000000000000002; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +yf18_prater_caught; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1929.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1929.05** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Josef把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Katharina Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Josef那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1929.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Klara说该自己走，Josef说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Josef摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.07000000000000006; health -0.030000000000000027 (在冰冷的楼梯上坐到半夜)_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-by-the-day` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Josef天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Josef把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。
  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; flag +day_labour_only; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1930.01** `yf18-runaway.north-station::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turn-back-at-the-barrier` tags: runaway, station, train, papers, escape, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef已经走到检票口，听见身后有人喊自己的小名。回头看见Katharina Bauer提着一只保温壶，站在卖报纸的摊子旁边。Josef把票递过去又收回来，最后陪她坐在长椅上喝完那壶汤；火车开走时，两个人都没有看站台。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 56 -> 36; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 21 -> 31; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.009999999999999787_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: flag +wish:craft:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004507_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Josef是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Josef在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 53 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 30 -> 32; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/someone-counts-the-savings` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Josef看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Josef第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 31 -> 46; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 36 -> 30; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1930.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lending.refused` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Josef说自己也等钱交房租，把口袋翻给Josef Huber看。Josef Huber没有再求，转身去敲另一扇门；第二天，楼道里有人说Josef见死不救，Josef听见了，只把煤筐往门里挪了挪。那天夜里，两家都早早关了灯。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; role; reputation -0.03; flag +loan_refused; knowledge “邻居转身去敲了别人的门，楼道里开始议论这件事。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Josef把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Josef没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 21; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 58 -> 56_

- **1930.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Grete Stein说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Grete Stein陪着Josef去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-grete-stein_

- **1930.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family, injury
  Josef和Luise Huber一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Josef去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Josef先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 49 -> 57; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 49 -> 53; reputation -0.03_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/carried-the-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef看见Emil Weiss在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Josef的腰在门框上撞了一下，Emil Weiss从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.030000000000000027 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1931.02** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  Josef把多出来的半锅扁豆汤卖给街角的鞋匠，收了两个十字币。Emil来晚一步，看着鞋匠端着汤上楼。Emil没说什么，但之后三天在楼梯上遇见Josef都只点头。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food_

- **1931.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Maria Stein的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Maria Stein在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Josef下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Maria Stein端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1931.06** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Gustav Moser没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.10** `kernel-social.neighbor-conflict::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/neighbor-conflict.coal-heap` tags: social, neighbor, conflict, everyday, housing, scarcity, reform, medical
  邻居把煤堆在走廊拐角，Josef的童车推不过去，只能把煤往墙边扫。邻居听见声响，出来说煤是自己的，谁动谁赔；Josef把扫帚靠在墙上，两人站在煤灰里，谁也不肯先回屋。Josef Bauer在楼下听见，拿粉笔在地上画了一道线。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; role; cash -1 (a new broom for the coal dust); flag +corridor_feud; knowledge “门房在走廊地上画了线，煤堆不得越过。”_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Mitzi Winter的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:ld18-public-desire` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  Josef的「被全城观看的欲望」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:183; flag -public_desire_active_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kin-nursed` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Hofer把粥熬得很稀，一勺一勺吹凉，托着Josef的后颈喂下去。窗帘拉开半尺，太阳从床脚移到墙上，Josef第一次觉得退烧后的骨头是轻的。Alois Hofer把药钱记在日历背面，说下个月少买两次肉。
  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; health 0.07999999999999996 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_kin_care; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1932.09** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/small-part` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, reform
  Elisabeth Huber只给了Josef三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Josef把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Josef，但乐队指挥记住了Josef每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:337; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:338_

- **1932.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josef Bauer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Alois Hofer把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Alois Hofer说不是Josef的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Josef心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1933.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Mitzi Winter没有答应，只说现在不合适。Josef把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005521_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, migration, unemployment
  Josef听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Josef把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Josef把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 42 -> 46; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 12 -> 15; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 55 -> 53; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/absorbed-again` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Elisabeth Huber的。Josef坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Josef发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 35 -> 41; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 50 -> 52; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 10 -> 8; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Grete Stein听完，说音准还行，但声音太薄，撑不起礼拜天。Josef问能不能先站在最后一排，Grete Stein说等下次有空位。回家的路上，Josef在无人的巷子里把那首歌唱完。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +choir_audition_rejected_

- **1933.02** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1933.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1933.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1933.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1933.09** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Josef把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Josef的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter)_

- **1933.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1933.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Josef学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。Elisabeth Huber嫌Josef变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Josef收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 55 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 8 -> 12; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 41 -> 37; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cup-mended` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把那只摔掉瓷的杯子放在Anton Winter面前，杯口用锡焊过，焊痕发黑。Anton Winter没有接，只问还记不记得为什么吵。Josef把那天的话原样说了一遍，说到一半停住，承认有一句是自己编的。Anton Winter这才把杯子转过来，看杯底的名字。
  _effects: cash -1 (粘回去的搪瓷杯); flag +repaired_friendship; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; knowledge “the quarrel began with a lie about money”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1934.04** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-as-living-exhibit` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  Josef在办公室承认自己连火柴头用什么配都不知道。Otto Winter 2沉默了一会儿，忽然问Josef愿不愿意每周三下午坐在展厅里，给参观者演示“发明家如何沉思”。酬劳是三克朗，外加一顶借来的礼帽。第一个周三，Josef坐了四个小时，只被一个孩子问过“你会不会变魔术”。
  _effects: cash 3 (扮演活展品的酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:427; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +hired_as_living_exhibit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef在最后一分钟把球顶进门，鞋钉在泥里划出一道沟。Alois Berg扑过来把Josef压在地上，观众席上有人把帽子扔进场。Josef Weiss站在场边，等Josef爬起来才说：明天照常上工，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 1 (最后一分钟的球); health -0.020000000000000018 (最后一分钟的球); reputation 0.06; flag +scored_winning_goal; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  Anton Winter 2把那张照片卖给了《晨报》，配文写“送别者的疲惫”。Josef收到两克朗模特费和一份报纸；照片里的Josef歪着头，嘴半张，确实像哭过。邻居开始叫Josef“那个在葬礼上睡着的人”，语气不算尊敬，也不完全是嘲笑。
  _effects: cash 2 (报纸给的照片使用费); reputation 0.01999999999999999; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:426; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +funeral_photo_in_the_paper; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006658_

- **1934.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Klara从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤)_

- **1935.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef看Klara盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Klara愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Josef门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Josef打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006661_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Alois Hofer把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Josef留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Josef在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Elisabeth Moser的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  _effects: cash -5 (赔偿和律师费（其中5克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; condition; flag +sued_over_a_vet_report; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  火车在清晨进站，Otto Winter 2把Josef的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Josef把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。

- **1936.12** `r1-hc.assault-knife::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::assault-crisis-dance-hall/assault-crisis-bottle` tags: street, assault, knife, violence, police, vienna, urban, crisis
  一个酒瓶在Josef脚边碎开，玻璃划进脚踝。Otto Winter说是失手，手却还攥着半截瓶口；Josef弯腰拔出玻璃，血把袜子染成深色，舞厅里的乐队没有停。
  _effects: cash -5 (舞厅后门的刀); health -0.16000000000000003 (舞厅后门的刀); condition; role; relation rival with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the man still held the broken bottle when he said it was an ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Josef没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Elisabeth Moser在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Berta Bauer。Josef在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Josef从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:528_

- **1937.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Josef和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Josef把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:529; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1937.01** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/burned-frame` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, vienna
  胶片在片门里起火，火苗顺着观察窗舔进大厅。Josef没有先去拿灭火沙，而是把后排的孩子往侧门推；Mitzi Weiss用湿毯子捂住片盒，手背烧掉一层皮。第二天，小巷电影院把烧黑的片盒留在柜台上，说“留着，提醒我们”。
  _effects: health -0.06 (burned while clearing the audience from the fire); condition; reputation 0.05; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:531; cash 1 (emergency pay for the fire shift); flag +cinema_fire; knowledge “硝酸片起火时，观察窗像一只被点燃的眼睛。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 37; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 51 -> 58; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 18 -> 17; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 65 -> 62; mood -0.040000000000000036; health -0.020000000000000018 (冬天阁楼里没有足够的煤)_

  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 65 -> 70; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 14 -> 18; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 39 -> 35; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  一年过去，Josef能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 22; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 65 -> 63; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef用面包和煤票请Emil Weiss每天来两次，Emil Weiss把药碾碎拌在粥里。Katharina Bauer退烧后第一句话是问面包够不够；Josef说够，转身把柜子里最后一块糖收进药盒。药盒盖上沾着面粉，她用袖子擦了擦。
  _effects: cash -2 (coal tickets set aside for the neighbour nurse); food; relation friend with person-emil-weiss; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; role; flag +nurse_paid_in_bread_

- **1937.09** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/moves-the-bed-in` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer在楼梯口喘了两次才上到三楼。Josef没有开会，也没有和谁商量，晚上就把折叠床搬进走廊尽头的小房间，把闹钟拨到凌晨两点。第二天，Josef在药盒上贴了第一张写着时间的纸。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:530; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +moved_parent_into_the_flat_

  Hermann Hofer在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Josef排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1938.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef去了Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，坐在后排听完了关于圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg经过数的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。

  Elisabeth Moser在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Elisabeth Moser把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Josef生日再讲。Josef先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:565; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000605:child; reputation 0.030000000000000013; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Maria Huber没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。

- **1940.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting

  _effects: historical development:initiative-taxi:person-alois-lechner-evt:world-vienna-1912:002575-1931 as participant; knowledge “我参加了第二区夜班出租合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/locks-changed` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, migration
  第二个星期，Josef下工回来，门锁已经换了。床架、锅和一只装票据的铁盒堆在楼道里，看门人Maria Moser 2把一张写有“自愿迁出”的纸递过来，说签了就能把东西搬进后院的共用间。Josef没有签，先把铁盒抱进怀里，手指被箱角划出一道口子。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; cash -9 (cart, storage and a replacement lock); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; role; flag +evicted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

  Gustav Lechner把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Josef把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000271:child; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1940.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把Elisabeth Huber和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Josef把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1941.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/auditor-is-the-beneficiary` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  审计开始前，Otto Kral把名片放在桌上：他是Maria Lechner 2的弟弟。Josef看着他翻开哥哥的考勤卡，又合上，说家里的事不影响审计。抽查只做了半天，报告只有一页，最后一页的签名是空的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; flag +auditor_was_beneficiary; knowledge “审计员的哥哥就是去年那个顾问，报告最后一页没有签名。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

  苏联士兵进城时，Josef躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Josef去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/advertises-in-the-paper` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Josef在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Franz Bauer放学后跟着Josef去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:836; flag +advertised_for_the_childs_medal; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1945.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/crowd-tears-notices` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef刚把告示刷上墙，就有人围过来说这是拿疫病吓人。一个卖菜的女人伸手把纸撕下来，浆糊蹭了Josef一脸。Katharina Hofer把Josef拉到一边，说这条街上个月才埋了两个孩子。
  _effects: health -0.04 (街角防疫告示的浆糊); reputation -0.03; flag +public_health_notice_torn_down; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002696:child; knowledge “the market crowd blames the notice for frightening customers”_

- **1945.02** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Josef在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001251:child_

- **1945.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工资袋上的油墨还没干透，Josef的手指染了一点黑。母亲把硬币倒在厨房桌上，一枚枚排开，排到第六枚时停住。灶上的水壶响了，她拿起来又放下，没继续问。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1946.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/says-it-was-only-metal` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Josef摸了摸空口袋，说那不过是块旧铜，丢了就丢了。晚上，Luise Bauer睡着以后，Josef又去电车站走了一趟，沿着白天的路线低头看每一块石板。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:896; flag +searched_the_tram_route_at_night; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag -something_is_missing; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.interview-worker::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/worker-talks` tags: knowledge, interview, worker, trust, journalism, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在车间后面的煤堆旁拦住Mitzi Winter 2，问加班费怎么算。Mitzi Winter 2先看了看门口，才说计件单价去年就降了，工头把差额记在“工具损耗”里。Josef把三句话重复了一遍，确认没有听错。
  _effects: flag +interviewed_fellow_worker; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123; knowledge “the piece rate was cut and the difference booked as tool wea”_

- **1946.06** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Alois Berg从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Josef买双能进厂的结实靴子。Josef数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子)_

- **1946.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Josef没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.029999999999999995; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

- **1946.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  夜里疼醒时，Josef摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Friedrich Berg第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Josef只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.019999999999999997 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

- **1947.01** `sm18-object.child-schoolbag::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/the-thimble-is-our-own` tags: child, school, object, accusation, misunderstanding, reputation, vienna, urban
  Grete Berg把顶针举到灯下，发现内圈刻着两个字：不是邻居家的姓，是Josef婆婆的姓。Josef这才想起，这只顶针是Franz Bauer从家里针线篮拿去学校做手工的，昨天还说过。教室里有人笑了一声。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; reputation 0.010000000000000002; knowledge “银顶针内圈刻着自家婆婆的姓，是孩子从家里拿去做手工的”; flag +thimble_was_our_own_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Maria Kraus这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 24 -> 19; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 57; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Josef又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 10 -> 16; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 0 -> 0; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `r13-work.collective-bargaining::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop::reform-eight-hour-clause/employer-stalls-on-hours` tags: work, union, bargaining, wages, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Huber 2说八小时是给官办工厂的，私人厂子按订单算。Josef把合同草案带回去，工人们围着看，没人愿意先签名。拖到第三周，厂里招了几个新工人，合同的事就搁在Mitzi Winter 2的抽屉里，和去年的那张放在一起。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +work13_hours_clause_stalled; knowledge “the employer is hiring around the eight-hour clause”_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Kraus这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 19 -> 22; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015578_

- **1947.06** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-a-secret` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Lechner把Josef在饭桌上说过的名字和地址卖给了别人。几天后，Josef发现自己的话出现在一封不该出现的信里。Maria Lechner没有否认，只说“大家都要活”。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002384:child; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:938_

- **1947.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Emil Lechner 2攥着Josef的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1947.11** `p9-work-transport.machine-speed/machine-speed-slow-belt` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, machine, speedup
  趁Mitzi Winter 2去账房送单子，Josef踩着垫木把皮带拨回慢槽。刚下地，Mitzi Weiss就在门口咳了一声——工头回来正好看见。第二天晨会上点了他的名字，记一笔，这个月的急活奖金没了。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Katharina Kral送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/denounced-to-police` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, unemployment
  旅店伙计记下了Josef和Friedrich Huber 3的名字。几天后警察来敲门，把两人带去问话；Josef丢了工位，罚了八克朗，Friedrich Huber 3的家里把人接走。Josef在档案里留下一条警察记录，手指上还留着问话时磕出的淤青。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; reputation -0.2; unemployment (dismissed after police visit); cash -8 (fine and lost work); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142_

- **1948.01** `fm17-saint.first-cure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-fever-breaks` tags: faith, healing, medicine, saint, investigation, child, reputation, money
  调查员刚走，隔壁的女人就把Berta Bauer抱到Josef门口。孩子烧得说胡话，Josef用凉水擦了擦他的额头，又把窗开了一条缝。天亮时，Berta Bauer的烧退了；女人在院子里说这是奇迹，Grete Moser却在台阶下站了很久，手里拿着没写完的记录。
  _effects: state_add fm17-saint-or-fraud state:world-vienna-1912:972; cash 1 (孩子母亲留下的谢礼); reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008381:child; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +fm17_first_cure_witnessed; flag +fm17_public_healer_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:fm17-saint-or-fraud/fm17-saint-inquiry` tags: fm17-saint-or-fraud, faith, healing, saint, fraud, reputation, religion, medicine
  调查员没有进院子，只在对街的咖啡馆坐了两个下午，数走了多少人、留了多少钱。Josef隔着窗帘看见他合上本子，知道那不是结束。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-saint-or-fraud.suspicion 20 -> 30; state_parameter fm17-saint-or-fraud.protection 10 -> 5; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015862_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Josef Kraus这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 11; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 57 -> 62; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Josef忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 0 -> 8; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 16 -> 14_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Josef谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 30_

- **1948.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lending.refused` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Josef说自己也等钱交房租，把口袋翻给Mitzi Lechner 2看。Mitzi Lechner 2没有再求，转身去敲另一扇门；第二天，楼道里有人说Josef见死不救，Josef听见了，只把煤筐往门里挪了挪。那天夜里，两家都早早关了灯。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; knowledge “邻居转身去敲了别人的门，楼道里开始议论这件事。”_

- **1948.06** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/leave-the-table` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Josef把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Alois Lechner耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Josef走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Josef没有停。第二天上工，Josef的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (一杯咖啡); flag +df18_refused_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “我拒绝了牌桌上的钱，但手指还记得那种感觉。”_

- **1949.01** `df18-omen.crows-on-the-wire::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ignore-the-birds` tags: omen, legal, investigation, crow, court, eerie, superstition, vienna
  Josef没有理那些乌鸦。开庭时，一个原本说没看见的证人改了口供，Alois Weiss 2把新证词夹进案卷。Josef坐在长椅上，听见自己的名字被念了两遍；窗外的乌鸦还停在电线上，像一排没有表情的旁听者。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 30 -> 45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “证人在开庭时改了口供。”; flag +df18_witness_changed_story_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef把Maria Kraus的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria Kraus今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 25 -> 33; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 57 -> 54; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一个孩子在被“祝福”后的第二天烧得更厉害。家里人没有闹，只把那条擦过额头的布巾留在门口；Josef把它捡起来，洗了，又晾回去，像是在等一个不会来的解释。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-saint-or-fraud.following 25 -> 20; state_parameter fm17-saint-or-fraud.suspicion 30 -> 40; cash -1 (请医生来家里看孩子); reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `p9-history-cultural-contact.join-circle/copy-programme` tags: culture, knowledge, letter, lecture, editor, training, circle, task
  Franz Weiss把草稿递过来，纸上有几处涂改。Josef在厨房抄到半夜，刀片刮纸的声音很轻。下星期四送去，Franz Weiss接过来看了看，放在桌角，没有说好不好。
  _effects: cash -1 (纸和墨水); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000694:child; skill; knowledge “文化圈每星期四在后厅聚会，节目单由成员轮流抄写”_

  电车从苹果车旁擦过，车辕断了，木箱扣在轨道上。Josef去扶车，被滚落的苹果砸中小腿，Emil先去看马，Hermann Stein 3已经在吹哨。那天下午，街上的孩子把没压烂的苹果捡得一个不剩，Josef坐在路沿上，裤腿全是果汁。
  _effects: cash -8 (电车轨道上的苹果车); health -0.019999999999999997 (电车轨道上的苹果车); condition; flag +apple_cart_overturned; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136_

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

- **1950.01** `kernel-civic.petition-drive::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-petition-lost` tags: politics, petition, reform, neighbor, organization, vienna, urban, postwar
  签名纸在电车上被挤掉一角，最后十个名字不见了。他站在终点站找了一刻钟，只在座位下找到一张被踩脏的纸；Friedrich Lechner说“算了”，他还是把剩下的名字重新抄了一遍。他把重抄的名单交给Friedrich Lechner，让对方再核一遍。
  _effects: flag +petition_signatures_lost; reputation -0.009999999999999981; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸和电车票); health -0.010000000000000002 (在终点站找纸); knowledge “签名纸要夹在硬纸板里，电车挤掉的一角找不回来”_

- **1950.08** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Luise Moser站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; reputation 0.03_

- **1950.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Gustav Moser 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Gustav Moser 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1950.12** `ad20-train-journey::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-compartment-sleeps` tags: travel, train, stranger, everyday, public, newcomer, vienna, urban
  车厢里暖得发困，Otto Weiss把窗户推开一条缝，又把带来的面包掰成两半。Josef接过一半，发现里面夹着一片薄薄的香肠。两个人没有交换姓名，到站时各走各的，Josef却记住了那片香肠的味道。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; food_

- **1951.01** `r2-leisure.mourning-dress::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::mourning-dress-reform/altered-ready-made-mourning-dress` tags: leisure, dressmaker, mourning, grief, craft, vienna, urban, postwar
  成衣店的黑裙第二天就送到，Hedwig穿着来改腰。Josef拆开下摆，发现里面缝着一块旧衬，针脚是殡仪馆的手法。Hedwig问要不要拆掉，Josef说拆了就不挺了，留着吧，反正没人看见。
  _effects: cash 3 (黑绸裙的最后一针); condition; flag +altered_ready_made_mourning_dress; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the ready-made mourning dress is lined with funeral-contract”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1951.01** `r13-fam.reconcile-parent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::parent-postwar/father-changed` tags: task, family, parent, reconciliation, estrangement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Bauer回来后不愿提营地的事，夜里常把窗子打开，说屋里太闷。Josef去送饭，Josef Bauer只问外面的粮价，不问Josef这些年怎么过。两人坐在桌边，一顿饭吃完，谁也没有提到“原谅”两个字。
  _effects: condition; flag +returned_parent_remote; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the parent sleeps with the window open”_

- **1951.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外是维也纳第十五区的一条窄街，卖烤栗子的炉子冒着白烟。Josef贴完纸退后两步看，发现“欠”字被浆糊洇开了一点。老成员没说话，只把眼镜摘下来擦了又擦。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004389:child; flag +union_ledger_public_

- **1951.03** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Josef自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

- **1951.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Josef把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Berta Moser放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Rosa 2在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002330:child_

- **1952.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.read-in-aisle` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Josef站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Alois Lechner 2在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Josef摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1952.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef数出八赫勒交给Alois Lechner，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Alois Lechner说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1952.05** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef对Anna Kraus说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Anna Kraus把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000905:child_

- **1952.09** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Alois Weiss提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1952.09** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1952.10** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001251:child; reputation -0.03_

- **1952.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1953.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1953.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stamp-on-credit` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Hofer把挂号纸推回来，说没有疾病保险的印花就不能看门诊。Josef数出几枚硬币，手在柜台上停住，后面的人绕过他往前挤，把窗口堵得更紧。Maria Hofer最后把印花本翻到最后一页，说先记在账上，月底一起收。
  _effects: cash -2 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); flag +clinic_fee_recorded_as_debt; knowledge “the outpatient fee can be recorded against next month’s wage”_

- **1953.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1953.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Grete Berg攥着Josef的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Josef在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

- **1953.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Grete Berg从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1954.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Grete Berg抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Grete Berg用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019685_

- **1954.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Josef的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019685_

  Maria Winter把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Maria Hofer第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Josef看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。

  战后新设的失业登记处在一间旧学校里，课桌当柜台用。Elisabeth Weiss 2问Josef战前做什么，又问他有没有被征召。Josef把退伍证明和工厂解雇单一起递过去，Elisabeth Weiss 2在两张纸之间来回看了三遍，最后批了十六周。
  _effects: cash 8 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_postwar_benefit_granted; knowledge “the postwar benefit runs longer for returning soldiers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1954.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Elisabeth Stein提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Josef把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; flag +parish_registered_

  Josef把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Josef折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。

- **1955.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Mitzi Lechner 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Mitzi Lechner 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。

  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Luise Moser把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef去铺子里告假。

  玻璃后面的招工纸被门房用尺子压平，Josef的名字写在横线本上，墨迹歪着。旁边的Mitzi Bauer凑过来看了一眼，说这厂子去年也招过两回，录用的多半是厂里老人的亲戚。
  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159_

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。

- **1957.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Josef的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price); health 0.04 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1929.09** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death

  _effects: death (肺炎); flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

### 215 subject events

- **1913.12** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Josef Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.05_

- **1915.01** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Emil一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Katharina Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Emil抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Emil哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1917.10** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Emil扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Emil摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Emil一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Anna Moser家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Anna Moser端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1918.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Hermann Kral正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Emil往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Helene Kraus的鞋掉在井台边，Emil先去捡鞋，回头才看见Helene Kraus摔在湿石头上。Anna Moser用围裙裹住Helene Kraus，血从膝盖渗出来。Josef Bauer夜里把Emil叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Emil留了半碗。
  _effects: health -0.019999999999999997 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_
