  教室的屋顶漏雨，课桌是用木板架在砖上的。Elisabeth Huber把Emil安排在炉子旁边，说新来的先管火。Emil一整天都在添柴，只学会了一个字：火。放学时Elisabeth Huber说，明天教你写自己的名字。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_school_stove; skill; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.06** `kernel-child.sibling-illness::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-illness-doctor-called` tags: childhood, family, illness, care, night, rural, agriculture, small_town
  半夜里Josef烧得说胡话，Emil被咳嗽声惊醒。Josef Bauer用湿布擦额头，又去敲邻居的门借电话，叫来的医生提着皮包上楼。诊费八克朗，药另算；Josef Bauer把过冬的煤钱数了两遍，还是签了欠条。欠条压在钟底下。
  _effects: cash -8 (doctor visit and medicine); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +saw_sibling_treated_

- **1919.08** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-chair-is-for-the-dead` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Emil把盘子摆好，说这把椅子是留给去年埋掉的那个人的。Josef Bauer手里的火柴停了一下，屋里只剩煤在炉膛里塌下去的声响。Emil又说，朋友不喜欢哭，所以今天谁都不许哭；说完自己先红了眼睛。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:34; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +friend_named_for_the_dead; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1919.11** `iw18-home.first-memory::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-kitchen-fire` tags: childhood, first_memory, fire, family, wonder, fear, rural, agriculture
  油布靠近炉边，先冒烟，随后窜起一小片黄火。Josef Bauer把一盆水泼上去，火没灭，反而顺着水边爬开；最后是湿麻袋压住的。Emil记得最清楚的不是火，是Josef Bauer光脚踩在地上、脚背上沾着黑灰的样子。
  _effects: health -0.010000000000000009 (a small kitchen fire); condition; cash -1 (a burned oilcloth and wet sack); mood -0.030000000000000006; flag +kitchen_fire_memory_

- **1920.01** `vienna-life-school-day/hungry-in-class` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Emil早上只喝了一碗清汤，第二节课时眼前发黑。Emil Weiss 3让孩子去走廊坐一会儿，给了半块面包；Emil把面包屑拢在手心吃完，胃里还是空的，但看Emil Weiss 3的眼神软了些，回到座位时把腰挺直了一点。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (went to school hungry); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1920.01** `iw18-friend.takes-the-blame::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-friend-did-it` tags: childhood, imaginary_friend, blame, family, secret, small_cruelty, rural, agriculture
  牛奶罐从桌沿滚下去，在砖地上碎成三瓣。Emil看着地上的白沫，说“是朋友碰的”。Josef Bauer没有立刻发火，只把碎片一片片捡进围裙；捡到最后一片时，才问朋友今天穿什么颜色的衣服。Emil答不上来。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 60 -> 68; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 65 -> 61; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 30 -> 36; cash -1 (a broken milk jug); mood -0.03_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Emil还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Emil知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Emil把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 65 -> 68; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 71 -> 73; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  湿煤灰写出第一笔时，Emil还挺高兴，写到第三行，手一蹭就全花了。Josef Bauer看见了，把自己的旧手帕撕下一角，蘸水给木板擦了底。第二天在Leopoldstädter Volksschule门口，同学指着那块发黑的木板问是什么，Emil说是石板，对方笑得弯下腰。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04000000000000001; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1920.05** `iw18-friend.sugar-cube::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cube-disappears` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, food, family, comic, rural, agriculture
  Emil把家里最后一块方糖放在空椅子旁边的碟子里，说是给朋友留的。下午糖不见了，Emil一口咬定是朋友吃了；晚上Klara从口袋里摸出黏着糖渣的纸，笑得直不起腰。Emil追着Klara绕了三圈桌子。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 61 -> 65; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 68 -> 71; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.11** `iw18-play.toy-theatre::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-stage-collapses` tags: childhood, play, theatre, family, wonder, comic, rural, agriculture
  演到第二幕，鞋盒的侧板忽然塌了，国王和仆人一起滑进床底。Emil愣了一秒，索性把塌下来的纸板当成地震，继续念台词；Josef Bauer笑得直拍床沿，最后趴在地上帮孩子找那只狗。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; skill; flag +toy_theatre_collapsed_

- **1921.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/fading` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Emil这一年很少再和那把椅子说话。学校里有了新的同桌，放学后要在楼梯间跳格子；有一天Emil把书包扔在椅面上，才想起自己已经三天没有给朋友留位置。椅面凉得很快，像一块普通木头。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 68 -> 60; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 36 -> 34; mood -0.009999999999999995_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Emil Kraus哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Emil蹲下把Emil Kraus驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Emil Kraus听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Emil的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Katharina Bauer回来接过孩子，Emil扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000726:child_

- **1921.12** `vienna-school-childhood.coal-sled/coal-sled.bruise` tags: school, social, childhood, injury
  Mitzi的帽子在排水沟里漂着，Emil的膝盖撞在砖堆上。Josef Bauer用湿布敷了敷，针线在裤子上走了两圈。煤渣坡的雪被压成了黑冰。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (雪橇撞上砖堆磕伤膝盖); mood 0.05; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Emil的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:34; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-borrowed-from-teacher` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  “借你可以，拿劳动换。”Grete Stein把旧石板推到桌沿，又指了指墙角的灰桶。Emil先去后院倒灰，风把炉灰吹回来，落在睫毛上。回来时Grete Stein已经用粉笔在石板背面写了一个“借”字，说擦掉那天就是还石板的日子。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; health -0.010000000000000009 (每天多留倒炉灰，沾上粉尘)_

- **1922.06** `vienna-school-childhood.fountain-pen/fountain-pen.broken` tags: school, social, childhood
  Katharina Bauer把账单翻了个面，上面全是数字。Emil没再问，回家用线把旧笔尖绑在木杆上。第二天墨水从裂缝渗到指缝，Emil Weiss 2把本子推回来让重写。
  _effects: mood -0.05000000000000002; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; skill_

- **1922.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Elisabeth Huber从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Emil答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Emil回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Elisabeth Huber看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Emil站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Josef Huber在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Emil掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Emil才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1923.08** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Emil的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Emil追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Helene Kraus从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000177:child; condition_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Emil把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Josef Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Emil都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1924.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Emil把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Maria Moser让Emil去区公所补。Emil跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Maria Moser看见Emil把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Emil赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Emil第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1925.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Emil在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Anton Winter 2跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Emil去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.11** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.lost` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Winter 2收了Elisabeth Bauer。Emil在门口站到天黑，回家把父亲的工具箱打开，照着记忆里的样子练。Katharina Bauer进来，说别跟你兄弟争，争来的东西不长久；Emil没有抬头，刨花落在脚背上，像一层薄雪。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +lost_apprenticeship_to_sibling; role; skill; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1925.12** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Otto Lechner先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Emil每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:96; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Emil把手套交给Elisabeth Bauer，让Elisabeth Bauer替自己去还。Elisabeth Bauer回来时说，Berta Hofer问是谁捡到的；Elisabeth Bauer顺口报了一个名字，Emil听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:97; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Emil翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Josef在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Emil翻回来时，Elisabeth Bauer把一支烟塞进Emil嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; health -0.030000000000000027 (cold and fear on the bridge); reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Emil又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Berta Hofer。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 37 -> 42; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 33; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  客户把床单贴在脸上，又摸了摸边角，说“还是潮的”，把门合上一半。Emil把床单卷回筐里，背回洗衣房。Friedrich Kral没骂，只让在后院重晾，件钱等重送完再结。Emil蹲在地上翻布角，晚风吹得膝盖发酸，天黑前才夹完最后一只夹子。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  Emil把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Elisabeth Bauer一样的白手套；Emil站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 37; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Emil把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Katharina Bauer醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Emil说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; flag +yf18_unpacked_

- **1927.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton在第二支曲子前把Klara请走，说Emil答应过要替她看管外套。Emil抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Emil把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 42 -> 47; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 33 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 15 -> 20; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000002; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 28 -> 31; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Katharina Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1927.08** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-country` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Emil没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Emil把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Emil说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family

  Anton Winter 2在班上念了Emil的作文，念到“她的手套放在木座上”时停了一下，说这就是考试里最危险的东西：把私事当成论据。全班笑了，Emil盯着桌面上的墨水圈，听见Elisabeth Bauer在最后一排合上了书。

  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Emil把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josef Bauer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:182; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  欠了兩個月学费，Friedrich Kral把Emil的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Franz Berg在门口拦住Emil，说学校不是救济院。Emil回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Katharina Bauer后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。

  Emil把票退了，和Elisabeth Bauer在普拉特门口的摊子上吃了两碗热汤。汤里有太多盐，Emil喝到一半就放下勺子；Elisabeth Bauer说等明年攒够钱再一起走，Emil点头，没有说自己已经把包袱收进了柜子最下层。

  Emil在炉子前把信烧了，纸灰卷起来，像一片黑叶子。Josef第二天问有没有话要说，Emil说没有。两个人还是一起走了一段路，在面包店门口分开，谁也没有回头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +burned_the_schoolbook_letter_

  Emil在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Berta Hofer三步远的地方拐进面包店。Emil买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  Emil在衣帽间堵住Josef，问那两张票怎么到了Klara手里。Josef说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Josef的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 63 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 47 -> 57; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 23 -> 18; reputation -0.02; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Anna Winter在第二支曲子前把Klara请走，说Emil答应过要替她看管外套。Emil抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Emil把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 58 -> 63; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 31 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 42 -> 47; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000155:child; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Emil上一个东家是谁。Emil说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Emil被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Emil站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07000000000000006; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Otto Winter 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Otto Winter 2在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1930.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sold-the-wrong-bottle` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Emil把一瓶玫瑰水当成古龙水卖给Maria Hofer，收了两克朗。第二天，Maria Hofer带着发红的脖颈回来，小巷电影院当着人把瓶子摔进垃圾桶，赔了一瓶新的。Emil被罚擦三天柜台，鼻尖一直留着玫瑰和酒精混在一起的甜味。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:259; cash -1 (paid for the returned bottle); reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “玫瑰水不能当古龙水卖，{customer}的脖颈第二天就红了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Emil 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Josefine Hofer 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1930.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil请楼下的Emil Weiss 2白天来照看Josef Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Emil Weiss 2上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Emil下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Josef Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1931.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/customer-allergy` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  Franz Lechner试了新到的紫罗兰香精，脖颈当天起了一圈红疹。Emil认出那批香精兑了工业酒精，追到门口把人叫回来，退了钱，又写了一张配方让药房配温和的。Franz Lechner后来带来一篮李子，说是自家树上结的。
  _effects: cash -1 (refunded the harsh violet essence); reputation 0.03; relation friend with person-franz-lechner; skill; flag +warned_allergy; knowledge “那批紫罗兰香精兑了工业酒精，闻起来甜，擦在皮肤上会起疹。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 15 -> 0; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 40 -> 30; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fixed-the-stove` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Winter家的炉子倒烟，Emil蹲在地上把烟道拆开，掏出一团堵了多年的煤灰。手背被铁皮划了一道，Helene Winter用旧布条缠上，说这炉子是老伴生前盘的。火重新旺起来时，屋里的人都往后退了半步。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (把煤筐抬上三楼); condition; flag +repaired_neighbour_stove; skill; relation friend with person-helene-winter_

- **1931.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Emil看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-alois-lechner_

- **1931.04** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Katharina Moser凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Emil把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1931.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Otto Lechner借给Emil一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Emil睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Berta Hofer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  面试在一间改作办公室的公寓里进行。小巷电影院把三把椅子摆成一排，自己坐中间，让Emil坐最边上。问到第三句，小巷电影院忽然说：“客人若认出你，你先介绍店，还是先介绍自己？”Emil说先介绍店；小巷电影院在纸上画了一个圈，又把它涂成实心。
  _effects: employment 店员 @ cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36-evt:world-vienna-1912:002344; skill; relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +hired_on_probation; reputation 0.02; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Emil还不上旧账，只好向Otto Winter 2介绍的放贷人再借十克朗，月息两成。Franz Winter收了旧账，又重新开了一页赊账；Emil在两本账上都按了手印。回家路上，Emil把两张借据分开藏，怕家里人看见。
  _effects: cash 10 (借新债还旧账); flag +new_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; knowledge “放贷人的月息是两成，借据要分开藏。”; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  _effects: cash 1 (合租省下一份房租)_

- **1933.05** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hired-as-living-exhibit` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  Emil在办公室承认自己连火柴头用什么配都不知道。Maria Moser沉默了一会儿，忽然问Emil愿不愿意每周三下午坐在展厅里，给参观者演示“发明家如何沉思”。酬劳是三克朗，外加一顶借来的礼帽。第一个周三，Emil坐了四个小时，只被一个孩子问过“你会不会变魔术”。
  _effects: cash 3 (扮演活展品的酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:384; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +hired_as_living_exhibit; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.02; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Emil开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work（其中3克朗记入债务）); health -0.03091666666666621 (reduced food)_

  Alois Stein把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Emil留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Emil在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018; role_

  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 30 -> 37; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000002; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil没掏自己的钱，先去了教区互助会，管事的人翻着册子答应补上差额，条件是要Maria Weiss在册子上按下手印。药费凑齐了，Maria Weiss把拇指在印泥上按了一下，红印落在名字旁边，像一枚小小的负担。
  _effects: health 0.04 (经由互助会拿到药); reputation 0.03; flag +parish_aid_arranged; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  Emil用湿布和半瓶退烧药熬到天亮，炉子多烧了一筐煤，锅里的粥也多添了两把面，口粮下去一截。Otto Lechner 2来时只在门口站了站，留一张饮食单，说继续观察，出诊费免了。Emil整夜没合眼，Anton Winter的烧慢慢退下去；桌上多了一张纸，炉边少了一筐煤。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Emil托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Katharina Bauer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Emil晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Katharina Bauer的腿伤有人料理，Emil却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.07999999999999996 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Elisabeth Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Elisabeth Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil想在午饭前把抽屉交上去，锯得太急，左角接缝处露了一道头发丝宽的光。Otto Winter把抽屉翻过来对着窗，拿小锤柄敲了两下，说这道缝进了潮气就会张。证明先不写，再干三个月，工钱照学徒算。Emil重新量线，锯子放慢了一半。

  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: cash 3 (晚宴前辞工); flag +retained_cook_in_crisis; role; knowledge “experienced cooks are scarce during the crisis”_

  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 3; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 59; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `r14-spec.hoax-newspaper::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-canals-of-mars` tags: hoax, newspaper, media, humiliation, fraud, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil把一篇“火星运河观测”的稿子放在Otto Lechner 2桌上，附了一张自己画的星图。Otto Lechner 2问证据在哪，Emil说天文学家太忙，没空否认。报纸加印两次，街上有人仰头看天，Hermann Hofer连夜排字，手指沾满黑铅。八克朗广告分成装进信封；三天后，Otto Winter在另一家报纸上写文章驳斥，反而让更多人买了头版。
  _effects: cash 8 (整版登出的火星人); reputation 0.05; flag +spec14_hoax_success; knowledge “the star chart was drawn around the rim of a coffee cup”_

- **1937.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Emil没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1937.04** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Emil把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Emil没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 19; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1937.10** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.04999999999999993; flag +watch_pawned; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Emil在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Otto Kral第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1938.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  Emil陪Luise Huber去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Luise Huber用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Emil没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1938.02** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Emil还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 3 -> 5_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008816_

- **1938.04** `p10-crisis-migration.eviction/eviction-shelter-queue` tags: adulthood, crisis, migration, war, police, eviction, refugee, housing
  看门人把锁芯拧下来的时候，院子里的孩子正围着床架转。Otto Kral帮着把立柜抬进屋，说先放着，别让雨淋。收容所的走廊有消毒水味，Alois Weiss 2在名册上一行行写，写完让他们在长椅上等天亮。
  _effects: residence; cash -2 (收容所登记与寄存箱子的杂费（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1938.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Anna Lechner说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Anna Lechner陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000595:child_

- **1938.07** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Emil在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/leave-vienna` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, migration
  姑母家的门开着，厨房里飘出汤味。Emil和Emil Lechner 2把箱子放在同一间屋里，十二克朗的车票钱已经花光；姑母没有多问，只添了两只碗。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; cash -12 (train tickets and lodging（其中12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

  一年过去，Emil能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 23 -> 27; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 14 -> 19; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 5 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 59 -> 63; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 5; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 63 -> 61; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Emil把半天工的工钱单交回去，换回自己看孩子。晚饭从两顿并成一顿，土豆和面包皮煮一锅，伴侣把煤钱数了两遍。孩子睡下后，Emil在窗边缝袜子，一枚针扎了手指，她含住指头继续缝。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入（其中3克朗记入债务）)_

- **1939.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Emil说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Maria Stein一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1939.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Franz Weiss，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Franz Weiss点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000694:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil没有立刻答话，先去敲Alois Berg的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Alois Berg提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Emil把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

  他被拒绝入境，理由是“没有固定住所”。他在边境站给Josef打电话，电话转到邻居家；对方说“先回来再说”，他买了一张反向车票。他在边境站的长椅上坐到天亮，才买下反向车票。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -5 (反向车票和电话费（其中2.6克朗记入债务）); flag +border_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “没有固定住址会被拒绝入境，亲戚的电话不能作为住址证明”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1940.03** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.09000000000000008_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010093_

- **1940.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil数出八赫勒交给Emil Weiss，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Emil Weiss说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Emil盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1940.04** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Emil把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Otto Lechner 2放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Mitzi在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1940.10** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-concert-ticket` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Emil不再让Anton Winter传话，托其带去一张星期天音乐会的站票，花了一克朗二十赫勒，位置在柱子后头。票上不写多余的字，只说若不方便，票留着也无妨。Anton Winter把票揣进衬衫内袋，Emil则把找回来的铜板一枚枚放进铁盒，等一个回音。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (音乐会站票一张); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  房产经纪人只用了两天就找到买主，要把烟铺改成一家卖进口香皂的店面。Emil在契约上签了字，拿到十二克朗，比继承来的赊账多出不少。Otto Lechner 2和她的姐姐在月底搬去城郊的侄女家，临走前把猫留在空柜台下面；Emil没有回去看那只猫，只在账本最后一页写下“已结清”。
  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; reputation -0.05; condition; flag +sold_the_tobacco_shop; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Emil能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 27 -> 31; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 19 -> 24; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 61 -> 59; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.soup-kitchen-crisis/crisis-locals-only` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, crisis
  有人在门口喊，说汤是给本区人喝的，不能便宜了外来的。Emil看了看锅里剩的底子，又看了看队伍里那些没票的人。Helene Bauer让Emil把门关一半，说先紧着孩子和老人。门板合上时，外面有人用德语和另一种话一起骂。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.04; flag +soup_kitchen_turned_away_outsiders; knowledge “the kitchen has no rule for outsiders and only enough for on”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1941.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil看Anna Weiss盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Anna Weiss愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Emil门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Emil打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: food_

- **1941.08** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Emil在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Mitzi Lechner 2的门口。傍晚Mitzi Lechner 2来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1941.12** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/bombed-out` tags: war, homefront, shortage, migration
  半夜的空袭把屋顶掀开一角。Emil和邻居把家具拖到楼道，天亮后去市政办事处登记损失。窗户用木板钉上，一家人挤到地下室。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; condition; cash -6 (repairs and moving)_

- **1942.01** `hs18-blackout.cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/trade-for-shelter` tags: blackout, cellar, coal, neighbor, denunciation, history, vienna, urban
  Emil没有告发，也没有白帮忙。Josef Weiss 2把两桶煤分出一桶，换Emil在搜查时把楼上的孩子领到后院。成交时两人都没有提“煤”字，只说“今晚借个火”。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:708; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; flag +traded_coal_for_shelter; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  又有人来问那天的事。Emil把同一段话重说了一遍，对方却只记住其中两个名字；走的时候，门没有关严，楼道里的风把纸吹得直响。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 45 -> 55; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_expire:mb17-newcomer` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Emil的「初到维也纳的人」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1942.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Emil整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Josef Bauer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Emil在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: relation kin with person-josef-bauer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱)_

- **1942.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Emil摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Grete Huber第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Emil只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1943.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-account` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Moser把几张钞票压在咖啡杯底下，说只要一段能上版的细节。Emil讲了钢琴、门洞和那件湿外套；对方记得很快，临走前问能不能用真名。Emil说用化名，记者笑了一下，说化名也得像真的。
  _effects: cash 4 (sold a witness account to a reporter); state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 2; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 55 -> 70; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 83; reputation 0.02; flag +sold_witness_account_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Emil忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Emil听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 83 -> 75; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `r13-work.deliver-goods::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rain-ruined-two-boxes` tags: commerce, delivery, transport, weather, bribe, vienna, urban, crisis
  半路下起雨，Emil把油布往箱子上盖，风却把一角掀开。送到时，最上面两只纸箱已经软了，里面的货染了水汽。店主把湿箱挑出来，从货款里扣了五克朗。Hermann Hofer说这条街的排水沟一堵，雨就专往板车上灌。
  _effects: cash -5 (板车上的十二箱); condition; flag +work13_delivery_ruined_by_rain; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1943.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Anton Moser排在前面，回头替Emil看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Emil用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000323:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/give-camp-bed-and-board` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil让Hermann Stein睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Emil把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +kin_staying_over; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1943.11** `p10-work-career.wage-shortfall/p10-work-career.wage-shortfall.refuse` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, deduction
  Emil把工资袋推回柜台，说不讲清楚就不收。第二天他被调去洗料间，工钱按日算，水汽熏得眼睛发红。Berta Moser路过门口看了一眼，没敢多站。
  _effects: employment 洗料间日工 @ cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36-evt:world-vienna-1912:002344; condition_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Emil又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 75 -> 77_

- **1944.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/ledger-on-table` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Emil把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Helene Kraus的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Emil在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +medicine_bill_settled_

- **1944.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.only-hot-water` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil没有开门，只从门缝递出一壶热水，说自家也挤不下。走廊里的箱子到半夜还堆着，孩子的哭声隔着门板一阵一阵。Emil坐在桌边没有点灯，第二天在楼梯间遇见时避开了目光。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000905:child_

- **1944.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Emil问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Emil住的那条街烧掉一半。Emil跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Emil忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Emil听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 77 -> 69_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/meet-at-church-door` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  两人谁也没提柜子，饭吃得客气而生分。临走时Emil提议：和不和好另说，每年忌日就在教堂门口见一面，站十分钟也好。Anna Kraus想了一下，说行，但别指望他进屋。Emil把这话记在心上，回去路上把没送出去的半包烟丝塞回口袋。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000905:child; flag +truce_with_sibling_

- **1945.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Emil把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1945.07** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage, injury
  警报响时Emil躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: health -0.02 (shelter conditions); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1945.10** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; flag +rent_installment_plan_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013646_

- **1946.01** `state_expire:hs18-street-witness` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street

  _effects: state_remove hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:708_

- **1946.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.negotiate-hours/negotiate-hours.half-day` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, hours, saturday
  工时表铺在柜台上，边角卷起。Emil指着星期六，第二区夜班出租合作社的手指在数字上敲了两下，说下午歇可以，工钱按日扣。礼拜六中午，他推门出来，街上的影子短得像另一来处。
  _effects: cash -2 (星期六下午工钱按日扣减（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (终于有半天空闲); relation employer with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.03** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; reputation -0.04; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.11** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Emil把照片铁盒交给Anton Winter抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）); health -0.05 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1947.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.relative-introduction/relative-introduction.lead-works` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, relative, introduction
  星期天的点心拆开纸包，Franz Lechner 3先问他这半年干了什么。听完没多说，只说明天一早跟他走。料场边那个工头话不多，让他搬了两趟木箱，点了点头。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000605:child; flag +trial_work_pending; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015666_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/flat-allocated` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  等了三年，住房局终于给Emil一张分配单：利奥波德城一栋战后修复的公寓，两间房，走廊和水龙头仍与隔壁共用。Anna Weiss帮着把床架抬上三楼，墙上的弹孔用石灰草草抹过。
  _effects: residence; cash -10 (deposit and haulage（其中10克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; role_

- **1948.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Ferdinand Lechner攥着Emil的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1948.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.03 (母亲的药钱被挪去还债，家里几夜没睡好); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1948.03** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Josef Weiss 3在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Josef Weiss 3在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1948.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.warn-the-latch` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil没传话，只在二楼门口站了一会儿，提醒Mitzi Weiss夜里把门闩好，说楼梯间有人爱嚼舌头。Mitzi Weiss点点头，把门闩推过去，发出很轻的一声。此后送活的人还是照来，闲话却渐渐没人接。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1949.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016275_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1949.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.cheap-picture` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Emil和Otto Lechner买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Emil把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1949.04** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Gustav Kral在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Helene Bauer扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Emil把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”_

- **1950.07** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Emil在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Friedrich Huber 3的门口。傍晚Friedrich Huber 3来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142_

- **1950.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Alois Winter 2用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Emil才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147_

- **1950.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anna Weiss嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Anna Weiss家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Anna Weiss那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000317:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

  Emil从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Hermann Hofer翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Emil把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
  _effects: cash -2 (租书店借小说的押金); skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Franz Kral听，Franz Kral摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1951.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Grete Bauer在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Emil把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1951.10** `p10-work-career.wage-shortfall/p10-work-career.wage-shortfall.itemize` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, deduction
  Emil把工资袋拿回柜台，请Anna Winter 2把扣款逐项写清楚。会计翻了翻簿子，只在纸条上写“材料损耗”，签了名。他把纸条折好夹进工资册，硬币在口袋里一路响到楼梯口。
  _effects: knowledge “厂里扣工资只写“材料损耗”，没有逐项明细”; cash -3 (被扣掉的三克朗（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000155_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018833_

- **1952.01** `r13-fam.propose-cohabitation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/keys-on-table` tags: task, family, cohabitation, household, courtship, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil把两把钥匙放在桌上，一把是自家门，一把是今天刚配的。Friedrich Lechner没有立刻拿，先问房东知不知道，又问邻居会不会说闲话。Emil说已经跟门房讲过，只说是表亲来住；Friedrich Lechner把钥匙收进围裙口袋，当晚就提来一只藤箱。
  _effects: cash -5 (两把钥匙放在一张桌上（其中5克朗记入债务）); flag +moved_in_together; role; knowledge “the porter was told the partner is a cousin”; household_

- **1952.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.pay-increase` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1952.11** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1953.01** `r13-fam.share-budget::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/separate-purses` tags: task, family, budget, money, household, trust, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Lechner不肯把钱并在一起，说各人的工钱各人管，房租对半分。Emil把已经分好的煤钱又推回去一半，铁盒留在桌上，谁也没有去拿。那天晚上，两人各数各的铜板，灯芯剪了两次。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_separate_money_

- **1953.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Berta Huber 2提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Emil把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

- **1953.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Kral嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Gustav Kral家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Gustav Kral那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1953.11** `fa17-studio.private-failure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/burn-it` tags: art, writing, failure, poverty, privacy, craft, plagiarism, vienna
  Emil把第三幕撕成四份，塞进铁炉，火苗先舔到的是标题。屋子里有一股纸灰和胶水混在一起的味道。Helene Kral在门外敲门催房租，Emil说再等两天，然后坐回桌前，开始写一个只有四页的短篇：一个男人在煤气灯下修一把永远修不好的椅子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:1189; skill; cash -1 (买新纸和墨水的钱); flag +burned_manuscript_

- **1954.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-the-shop` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  房产经纪人只用了两天就找到买主，要把烟铺改成一家卖进口香皂的店面。Emil在契约上签了字，拿到十二克朗，比继承来的赊账多出不少。Elisabeth Winter和她的姐姐在月底搬去城郊的侄女家，临走前把猫留在空柜台下面；Emil没有回去看那只猫，只在账本最后一页写下“已结清”。
  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/another-rejection` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  画店老板把Emil的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Emil抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 60 -> 67; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 8 -> 7; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 75 -> 72_

- **1954.01** `r14-soc.gamble-away-wages::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lost-the-week` tags: task, gambling_debt, gambling, money, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  赛马场外的泥地上，Helene Bauer把Emil的工钱数进帽子里，说“下周翻本”。Berta Moser拽了拽Emil的袖子，Emil没有动。回家的电车上，Emil把口袋翻了三遍，只摸到一张赛马票的存根。第二天上工，Emil把工票藏在鞋垫下面，怕家里翻出来。那天夜里，Emil把赛马票的存根烧了，灰留在鞋垫上。
  _effects: cash -12 (第三个栏架前的马（其中12克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +gambling_debt; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000873:child; knowledge “the bookmaker takes bets behind the tram depot”_

- **1954.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Emil把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001249:child; skill_

- **1954.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把Helene Kral和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Emil把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1955.01** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  玛丽亚在市场拱门下拦住Emil，声音不大但周围人都听得见：三个克朗。Emil翻遍口袋只摸出几个十字币。玛丽亚把布袋从Emil胳膊上解下来，转身走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.15000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

- **1955.01** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `r13-fam.teach-parent-to-read::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::teach-parent-reform/night-class` tags: task, care, literacy, parent, school, intergenerational, vienna, urban
  市政厅办起了工人夜校，Emil替Josef Bauer去报名。管登记的人问年龄，Emil报小了五岁；Josef Bauer在旁边扯他的袖子，最后自己说“四十七”。第一晚回家，老人把发的铅笔用布包好，说老师夸Josef Bauer握笔稳。
  _effects: cash -1 (在厨房桌上写自己的名字); flag +enrolled_parent_in_night_school; role; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the adult night school takes workers after the evening shift”; skill; condition_

- **1955.03** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1955.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.half-bag-with-note` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil只舀出半袋，说自家也不宽裕，要Helene Berg 2把上次的煤钱一并记在纸上。Helene Berg 2接过纸片，当着面把日期和数目重写一遍，两人各留一半。面粉少了，账却清楚了，Emil把纸片夹进账簿样的旧本子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Emil把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Emil把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 55 -> 59; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 7 -> 10; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 67 -> 65; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `r13-fam.share-budget::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pooled-wages` tags: task, family, budget, money, household, trust, vienna, urban
  Emil把这个月的工钱倒在桌上，分成房租、煤、面包和一小堆应急的铜板。Friedrich Lechner把自己的钱也推过来，只留下车票钱。两人把钱数了两遍，数目对上了，Emil才把那只装钱的铁盒推到床底下，钥匙交给Friedrich Lechner。
  _effects: condition; flag +pooled_household_wages; role; knowledge “the household now keeps one emergency tin”_

- **1956.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Grete Berg起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.join-club::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/voice-rejected` tags: task, social, club, choir, organization, belonging, vienna, urban
  Emil唱到第二段就被Anna Berg 2打断，说声音太直，会把整排带跑。Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的人没有笑，只是把歌本收回去。Emil在门口等到散场，问能不能先搬椅子、烧炉子；Anna Berg 2说缺的是嗓子，不是手。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; knowledge “the choir chooses by voice, not by willingness”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

- **1957.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.pay-increase` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-167_

- **1957.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Elisabeth Moser说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Elisabeth Moser陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000605:child; mood -0.08000000000000007_

### 215 subject events

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anna Moser把Lena拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Lena把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Hofer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt门口，Lena蹲下把两包并排，按Anna Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill_

- **1918.06** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Lena一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josefine Hofer听见哭声跑出来，把Lena抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Lena哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Moser从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Lena。Lena把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Lena把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Lena踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Lena单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Lena站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Grete Bauer在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Lena掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Lena才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1924.01** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-bring-younger-neighbor` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Lena拉上隔壁更小的Anton一起去桥下，回程时那孩子的母亲堵在院门口，说Lena带坏了她家孩子，声音大得半条街都听见。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.01_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Lena解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Maria Huber 2也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Lena自己晾。Lena把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Maria Huber 2说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Maria Huber 2哼了一声，算是记下了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.04000000000000001; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1925.09** `statistical_infection`
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Lena从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1925.11** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Friedrich Kral说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josefine Hofer来签字。Josefine Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Lena把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/worked-for-dues` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Lena翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1926.02** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Maria Huber 2把成绩单按在讲台上，说Lena还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Lena签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); education apprenticeship_

- **1926.05** `r1-fam.schooling-decision::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/school.apprenticeship` tags: family, education, apprenticeship, poverty, future, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Stein把学徒合同摊在桌上，说三年管饭，出师后一天两克朗。Elisabeth Huber说Lena的算术是班里最好的，再读两年可以考师范。Josefine Hofer把合同推回去，说家里等不起两年；Lena看着窗外，操场上有人在踢一只破球。
  _effects: cash 2 (学徒合同还是课本); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; knowledge “老师认定这个学生能考上师范。”; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1927.01** `kernel-edu.apply-for-scholarship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-apply-scholarship-awarded` tags: task, adolescence, school, scholarship, papers, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把成绩单、洗礼证明和Josefine Hofer的收入证明摊在桌上，Grete Stein逐张检查，缺一张就划掉重来。Franz Winter在窗口盖了三个章，收下两克朗印花税。六周后，通知来了：每年二百四十克朗，先发三十。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 28 (first scholarship payment after the stamp fee); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +scholarship_awarded; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1927.03** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Lena把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josefine Hofer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:157; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  Maria Huber 2在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Lena下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); education secondary; skill; health -0.030000000000000027 (白天上工夜里上课睡眠不足)_

- **1927.06** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Huber先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Lena每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04000000000000001; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:156; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.10** `r2-leisure.cinema-fire::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::cinema-fire-reform/reported-blocked-fire-exit` tags: leisure, cinema, fire, panic, rescue, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena在检查那天看见消防水带被挪去压幕布，检查员一走，又挪了回去。Lena把这事告诉Maria Weiss，Maria Weiss说别多事，老板刚交了罚款。当晚电影放到一半，Lena一直盯着侧门，手心里全是汗。
  _effects: mood -0.06; reputation 0.02; condition; flag +reported_blocked_fire_exit; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; knowledge “the manager moved the fire hose back behind the screen”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, injury
  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Lena留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework); health -0.010000000000000009 (cramped home work)_

- **1928.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/call-the-inspector` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  Lena趁普拉特动物园夜班队去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Maria Moser看。Maria Moser第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；普拉特动物园夜班队陪着笑，等门一关，就把Lena的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。
  _effects: relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; reputation 0.03; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 5 -> 8; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 45 -> 57; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 15; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Mitzi Weiss就从楼梯口上来，盯着Lena看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Mitzi Weiss当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Hedwig的雇主。Lena站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Hedwig问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1928.02** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/hid-letter-in-bookshop` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena没有寄信，把信塞进Alois Lechner常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Josef Huber说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Lena去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1928.06** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  地理课上，Lena在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Lena认出是Josef Lechner的笔迹，趁Maria Huber 2转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Lena把地图册塞进Josef Lechner的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:186; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000079:child; flag +yf18_atlas_note; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1929.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Lena翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Hedwig在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Lena翻回来时，Anton把一支烟塞进Lena嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 30 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; health -0.03 (cold and fear on the bridge); reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:157; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Lena在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Josef Lechner。这一次对方先点了头，Lena准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Lena才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 45 -> 53; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000079:child; mood 0.03_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/teacher-explains` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena在办公室门口站到下课，才敢把作业本递进去。Emil Weiss 2翻到错得最多的那一页，没有骂，只让Lena把题目再读一遍。读到第三遍，Lena忽然明白自己一直看漏了条件。Emil Weiss 2把红笔放下，说以后不懂就来。那天以后，Lena的作业本上多了几行红字，都是Emil Weiss 2夜里改的。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1929.08** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/evening-only` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, reform, medical, schooling, home, family
  学校没有多余名额，只给Lena留了夜校的位置。白天在工厂站一天，晚上再去教室坐三个钟头；老师说先试试，撑不住也不算丢人。

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

  _effects: relation partner with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06_

  _effects: flag +pedagogy_dispute; skill_

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Ferdinand 3。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Franz Lechner 3把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:468_

- **1935.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/chimp-on-the-prater` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, vienna
  黑猩猩“卡斯帕”用一根弯钉挑开第二道门，顺着排水沟跑进普拉特。Lena提着一桶苹果追到旋转木马后面，看见它坐在售票亭顶上，正把一张张票根往嘴里塞。Berta Huber赶来时，Lena已经用苹果把它引进了送货马车。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 14 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 25; cash -1 (apples used to lure the chimpanzee（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; flag +monkey_returned; knowledge “卡斯帕会用弯钉挑门闩，苹果比网子管用。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Lena已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Lena也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Lena被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission（其中13克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Franz Moser。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Anton Moser把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:898_

- **1946.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/keep-it-for-the-children` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  Hedwig想把钱拿去盘下街角的小酒馆，Lena却把十五克朗直接交到学校，替孩子续了下一年的学费。Hedwig把餐巾摔在桌上，说这家人永远只会把钱埋进土里。Anton Moser没有劝，只把汤碗往Lena那边推了推。
  _effects: cash -15 (school fees paid from the windfall（其中14克朗记入债务）); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 18 -> 3; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 62 -> 74; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 5; flag +fortune_kept_for_child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Lena侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.05999999999999994; skill_

- **1946.07** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/admitted-with-papers` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  火车在清晨进站，Otto Lechner把Lena的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Lena把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。
  _effects: cash -2 (the passport fee and the first week's rent（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:899; state_add mb17-remittance-web state:world-vienna-1912:900; role; flag +mb17_admitted_at_border; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “临时居留纸要求在四十八小时内到区公所登记住址。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; flag +mb17_newcomer_active; flag +mb17_remittance_web_active_

- **1946.09** `r1-hc.pickpocket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pickpocket-loses-wages` tags: street, pickpocket, tram, theft, wages, vienna, urban, postwar
  电车上人挤人，Lena感觉外套被人轻轻拽了一下。到站摸口袋，钱包已经没了，里面是半个月的工钱和一张房租收据；收据上的地址，现在只剩她自己记得。
  _effects: cash -10 (电车上的第三只手（其中10克朗记入债务）); flag +lost_wages_to_pickpocket; role; knowledge “the tram crowd presses close at the second stop”_

- **1947.01** `hs18-blackout.bakery::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/sell-matches` tags: blackout, queue, bread, rumor, neighbor, shortage, vienna, urban
  Lena在门口卖火柴，一盒顶平时三盒的价。买的人不少，有人划亮一根照路，又舍不得扔掉。Mitzi Moser从柜台后喊“别在店里点火”；Lena把剩下的火柴塞进包里，觉得这钱赚得比面包还快。
  _effects: cash 3 (sold matches at blackout prices); state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.panic 27 -> 35; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +sold_matches_during_blackout; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/still-a-guest` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Lena又搬了一次房，箱子里多了一把缺齿的梳子和一叠没寄出的信。新住处离电车线更远，邻居说德语很快；他只能点头，等对方走后再把门关上，把今天听错的词一个一个记在旧票据背面。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 19 -> 20; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 9 -> 7; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 65 -> 69; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.postcard` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  问询表要出生日期和最后通信地址，Lena写到一半停了笔，把哥哥的信从证件夹里翻出来照着抄。寄出去后每天看门房有没有信。两个月后明信片送到，画面是鲁尔河，背面铅笔字被雨水洇过。
  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费（其中1克朗记入债务）); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1947.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Lena在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  Lena没有开门，只从门缝递出一壶热水，说自家也挤不下。走廊里的箱子到半夜还堆着，孩子的哭声隔着门板一阵一阵。Lena坐在桌边没有点灯，第二天在楼梯间遇见时避开了目光。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1947.11** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/hearse-as-taxi` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Lena拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Emil Kraus把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Lena没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Gustav Lechner起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:939; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000271:child; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.11** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Lena把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Lena没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 19; mood 0.010000000000000009_
