  Hedwig刚开口，Franz Weiss就把报纸举高，说这张桌子有人。Hedwig端着杯子在门口站了一会儿，回去把椅子放正；第二天同一时辰，Franz Weiss没有来，桌上留着一圈没擦干的杯印。
  _effects: flag +rebuffed_while_seeking_friend; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000694:child_

- **1950.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Hedwig靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

- **1950.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Hedwig把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005386:child; skill_

- **1951.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Josef Weiss 3指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1951.04** `ld18-fitting.measurement::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-dress-is-finished` tags: love, desire, erotic, craft, queer, fitting, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Hedwig把婚纱熨得没有一道褶，交活时只收了料钱。Rosa Winter在门口回头，说谢谢；Hedwig说祝您幸福。门关上后，她把剩下的缎带缠在手指上，缠得太紧，指节都白了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002100:child; cash 2 (the wedding dress was paid for); flag +unspoken_fitting_desire_

- **1951.10** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Hedwig用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Maria Winter翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Alois Lechner在走廊里问交了多少，Hedwig含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

- **1952.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hired-as-hand` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, postwar
  铁门只开了一条缝，Franz Kral把人数到一百二十，才放最前面十个进去。Hedwig把冻裂的手摊在灯下，指节上的茧替她答了话。Franz Kral在纸角写了个“明早六点”，Alois Weiss 3被推到一边，脚边的麻袋口还开着，土豆滚进了排水沟。
  _effects: cash 2 (铁门开了一条缝); flag +work13_hired_at_factory_gate; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; employment Tagelöhner @ org:world-vienna-1912:受监管的工人合作社:953; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159_

- **1952.05** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Hedwig把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Ferdinand 2递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.03 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004852:child_

- **1952.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Josef Weiss 3的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Josef Weiss 3提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1952.10** `r1-work.porter-luggage::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lost-the-suitcase` tags: work, porter, station, luggage, accusation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig把十七号牌交给Mitzi Weiss，转身去搬下一件，回来时箱子已经不在架子上。Grete Moser说牌子对不上，责任在Hedwig。Mitzi Weiss把车票拍在柜台上，Hedwig赔了六克朗，那是半个月的饭钱，数到最后一张时手停了。
  _effects: cash -6 (行李房里的十七号牌); reputation -0.06; flag +lost_luggage; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1953.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018911_

- **1953.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Hedwig在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Hedwig的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1953.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-aspirin-substitute` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  药房说没有货，Hedwig把处方叠好，去了下一家。天黑时手里只有一包阿司匹林和一瓶咳嗽糖浆；Gustav Moser 2说总比空手强，把糖浆让给了孩子。回家后他把阿司匹林分成两半，糖浆瓶口用纸塞着，漏了一点。
  _effects: cash -8 (aspirin and cough syrup after a day of searching); health -0.02000000000000008 (the prescribed medicine was not found); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1953.04** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Berta Huber追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1953.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Josef Weiss 3一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Josef Weiss 3的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1954.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Josef Weiss 3抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Josef Weiss 3用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019681_

- **1954.01** `kernel-work.task-wage-claim::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/work-wage-claim-retaliated` tags: task, work, wages, claim, deduction, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig拒绝在扣款单上签字，第二天被调去洗料间，工钱按日算，月底只剩二十克朗。Maria Bauer 2说这是“临时帮忙”，可班表上再没有Hedwig原来的位置。Hedwig把扣款单和调岗单钉在一起，交给工会干事；干事说可以申诉，但要等三个月。
  _effects: employment Waschhausarbeiter @ org-winter-print; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; role; flag +wage_claim_refused; condition; cash -2 (调岗后的工钱差额)_

- **1954.06** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

- **1954.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (鞋底不平磨出水泡); skill_

- **1954.09** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Hedwig在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。环城大道写字楼管理处把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1954.10** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Emil Lechner 2敲了门。Hedwig掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Emil Lechner 2什么也没说就走了。夜里Hedwig听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1955.01** `r14-hist.reconstruction-housing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/waitlisted-for-two-years` tags: reconstruction_housing, housing, postwar, repair, vienna, urban, reform, scarcity
  Grete Moser在等待名单上写下Hedwig的名字，编号在四百名以后。Hedwig搬进一间临时隔出的房间，厨房和厕所与另一家共用，夜里能听见隔壁的收音机。两年里，Hedwig每月去问一次进度，Grete Moser每次都翻到同一页，说“今年动不了，明年再看”。
  _effects: cash -2 (废墟上的三层砖); mood -0.08000000000000007; flag +housing_waitlist; knowledge “the housing list is more than four hundred names long”_

- **1955.07** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/license-it-and-keep-the-patent` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  Hedwig没有卖断，而是和第二区夜班出租合作社签了按瓶抽成的许可：每卖出一千瓶，付两克朗。第一年只卖了四千瓶，Hedwig拿到八克朗，却保住了专利。Alois Weiss 3在许可书上盖了一个小章，说这种条款以后会值钱；Hedwig把章看了一会儿，没敢笑。
  _effects: cash 8 (first royalty payment on the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1275; skill; flag +licensed_patent; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.11** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-take-it` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Hedwig把少了钱的工钱袋塞进内袋，没再问。回家路上买了两块面包，比平时少一块。
  _effects: cash -1.8 (工钱被扣); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165_

- **1956.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/the-inspector-was-an-old-friend` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Emil Kraus进门后没有先翻账本，而是问Hedwig是不是在战前服过役。两个人对上了部队编号，Emil Kraus把帽子放在桌上，说利息可以按小额储蓄申报，罚款就免了。Anna Kraus松了一口气，手里的计算尺却还停在半空，像是不知道该不该落下去。
  _effects: cash -2 (the reduced tax assessment); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 20 -> 18; flag +tax_audit_friendly; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.01** `r13-work.ask-raise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/piecework-instead` tags: work, wages, negotiation, workshop, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anna Kraus听完没有加钱，只把计件单推过来，说多干多得。Hedwig算了算，要做到天黑以后才抵得上两克朗。Grete Stein 2在旁边低着头打算盘，算珠碰得很轻。Hedwig把计件单折好，塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 3 (账房窗口前的两克朗); health -0.03999999999999998 (账房窗口前的两克朗); condition; flag +work13_took_piecework_instead_of_raise; knowledge “the master prefers piecework to a raise”_

- **1956.02** `sm18-object.heirloom-missing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/reported-before-the-will-is-read` tags: object, loss, inheritance, family, suspicion, everyday, vienna, urban
  律师刚走，Lena就在祖母的针线盒里发现少了一只银胸针。Hedwig记得最后一次见它是在遗嘱签字那天，胸针别在祖母的灰披肩上。Lena说先别声张，免得帮佣听见；Hedwig却已经把空盒子端到了厨房门口。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:1316; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +heirloom_reported_missing; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1956.12** `p10-family-fate.separation/separation-move-out` tags: adulthood, family, marriage, birth, death, inheritance, household, housing
  钱摊在桌上，铜币和纸币分开数，数到一半谁也不再数。Elisabeth 2借口借火，站在门口看了一眼就退回去。午后阳光照到床板，两床被子只剩一床。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000155:child; cash -5.5 (分给伴侣带走的那一半存款)_

  Hedwig的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Hedwig看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_

  Grete Moser翻完最后一页，说没有这样的东西，但记得上个月有个男孩来问过同样的问题。Hedwig留下地址，走出门又折回来，把地址栏里的门牌号改成了后院的小门。

  Hedwig把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Hedwig忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 10 -> 18; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 35 -> 33_

  Hedwig把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Friedrich Lechner 3在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Hedwig站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。

  Berta Huber在门口替Hedwig捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Hedwig没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Hedwig，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。

### 419 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Josef Bauer不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Josef Bauer拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Josef Bauer的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1913.01** `major-family.separation-widowhood/separation-widowhood.separate-smaller-room` tags: family, separation, widowhood, housing, major, lifecycle, household
  Josef Bauer把Katharina Bauer的礼拜日外套叠好，放在门房。两人去市政办事处问过，办事员说没有哪张表格能写清这种事；神父也不肯在文书上签字。Katharina Bauer把铺盖搬到工友的合租房，Josef Bauer退掉Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt的大房间，在同一栋楼里租了一间后屋，把结婚照留在原来的墙上。
  _effects: household; relation_end partner with person-katharina-bauer; residence; role; cash -10 (moving costs and the old room rent); mood -0.08_

- **1913.01** `r13-fam.join-club::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/signed-the-roll` tags: task, social, club, choir, organization, belonging, vienna, urban
  Anna Moser把歌本塞进Josef Bauer手里，说第二排缺一个唱中音的。Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的屋子煤味很重，钢琴缺了两个键，Josef Bauer还是跟着唱完了整首。散场时，Anna Moser让Josef Bauer在下星期的点名册上写名字，说来了就得来满一个冬天。
  _effects: cash -1 (合唱团第二排的空位); flag +joined_choir; role; skill; relation friend with person-anna-moser; organization_membership_

- **1913.03** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/wait-with-wet-cloth` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily, injury
  Josef Bauer用湿布和半瓶退烧药熬到天亮，炉子多烧了一筐煤，锅里的粥也多添了两把面，口粮下去一截。Emil Weiss来时只在门口站了站，留一张饮食单，说继续观察，出诊费免了。Josef Bauer整夜没合眼，Klara的烧慢慢退下去；桌上多了一张纸，炉边少了一筐煤。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (整夜守候，睡眠不足); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Josef Bauer的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef Bauer看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000049_

- **1914.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/room-rented` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, vienna, urban, peace
  Katharina Bauer把招租纸从门上揭下来，领Josef Bauer看了后间。屋里只有一张铁床和一只裂了口的洗手盆，窗户对着天井。Josef Bauer付了六克朗，Katharina Bauer把钥匙从围裙里解下来，说晚上十点关大门。
  _effects: cash -6 (新城区门牌下的招租纸); flag +lodging_secured_in_new_district; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the front door is locked at ten every night”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1914.09** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/leave-before-the-end` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  朗读开始不久，Josef Bauer 发现后排有人在记名字，便提前离开。走到门口时，Karl Kraus 的声音还在背后，句子像钉子一样短。第二天，Josef Bauer 在报摊上看到同一篇文章被删去了两段。
  _effects: knowledge “Café Central 的公开朗读吸引便衣和报纸记者，现场听众并不全是同情者。”; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; cash -1 (coffee before leaving the reading); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1915.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.invite-to-theatre/unsigned-note` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  开口成了问路，对方指完方向就转身去整理那叠乐谱。当晚有人坐在租来的房间里，把一张信纸写了撕、撕了写，最后只剩一句“星期天下午剧院有楼座”，没有署名，信封上写了书店的地址，邮票六赫勒。第二天投进街角的邮筒，此后三天经过书店都绕着走。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (邮票和信纸); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1915.05** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  Josef Bauer把腌鲱鱼放在黑面包上，咸味够配一大杯水。Alois Lechner拿着盐罐过来，看见鱼就说拿卷心菜换。换完以后，两家灶上都多了一样东西。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1915.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/open-the-books-again` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef Bauer把账本摊在面粉袋上，一栏一栏念，念到某笔去向不明时屋里静了。老人把手套戴上又摘下，Rosa Lechner拿回自己那份登记，说先按墙上的数领。雪还在下，门一直开着。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; skill_

- **1916.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Josef Bauer把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Emil Weiss在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Josef Bauer站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07; relation friend with person-emil-weiss; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Josef Bauer 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Alois Lechner 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books and enrolment fee); role; flag +enrolled_in_school; knowledge “课本钱要另付，每个孩子两个克朗。”_

- **1918.07** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-mine-was-salted` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, rural, agriculture
  抽水机运到山里才发现，矿洞的岩壁上早被人撒过金粉，岩芯也是挑过的。Emil Weiss在两个月前把股份转给了一家空壳公司，办公室只剩一张桌子和半瓶墨水。Hedwig Stein把调查报告放在Josef Bauer面前，最后一页写着“建议不予起诉”，因为找不到被告。
  _effects: cash -50 (the investment in a salted mine); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:28; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; condition; flag +salted_mine; knowledge “矿洞岩壁上的金粉是撒上去的，岩芯也被挑过。”; mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1919.01** `ml18-reversal.old-partner-returns::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/forgive-and-rebuild` tags: reversal, money, betrayal, business, scam, comeback, rural, agriculture
  Hermann Kral在咖啡馆角落坐到Josef Bauer喝完第二杯，才把一只旧皮包放在桌上：里面是当年那笔投资的全部账目，还有一张新的合伙契约。Josef Bauer看了很久，最后在契约上改了分成比例，把第一年的利润留给Luise Moser。Hermann Kral笑了一下，说这不像Josef Bauer，又像。
  _effects: cash 6 (the first payment under the new partnership); state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -55 -> -40; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 55 -> 40; relation friend with person-hermann-kral; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +rebuilt_with_old_partner; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  Josef Bauer和Anna Bauer的书都缺页，两人把笔记摊在咖啡馆后桌，交替着抄，谁先到谁先占座位，一杯咖啡续到打烊。结课前一夜复习到十一点，Maria Huber 2第二天发卷子时扫了一眼他们的笔记，说抄得比听讲还整齐。Josef Bauer考了七十八分，结业证照发，只是名次靠后。

- **1922.03** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Otto Lechner把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef Bauer把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Otto Lechner低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1922.04** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Josef Bauer手里还捏着念珠，Helene Winter挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1922.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Bauer正要把空床许给Josef Weiss，Emil Weiss却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Josef Bauer回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Josef Weiss，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  Josef Bauer和Emil Weiss 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Josef Bauer去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Josef Bauer先垫了药钱，说以后再说。

  Josef Bauer在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Franz Stein把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); health -0.030000000000000027 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Josef Bauer嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Josef Bauer用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; reputation 0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  Josef Bauer下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; skill_

- **1925.05** `r1-fam.friendship-formation::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friendship.bucket` tags: social, friendship, neighbor, water, trust, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Bauer和Luise Bauer因为一只水桶吵了一架，谁也不肯先开口。过了半个月，Luise Bauer家的孩子病了，Josef Bauer把自家的退烧药分了一半放在她门口，没有留名字。第二天，水桶被放回了原处，桶底多了一块洗得发白的布。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +reconciled_over_a_water_bucket; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  后厅的椅子不够，Josef Bauer 站在柱子旁听Karl Kraus 读一篇关于报纸谎言的文章。Karl Kraus 读到一半停下来，指出台下某位记者上周写的句子，那人把帽子压低。散场后，Josef Bauer 在账单背面抄下一句，墨迹被咖啡杯底印开。
  _effects: knowledge “Karl Kraus 在咖啡馆公开朗读时，会逐句引用报纸原文并指出其中的修辞和谎言。”; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; cash -2 (coffee and a seat at Café Central); mood 0.020000000000000018; skill_

- **1926.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Josef Bauer翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 45 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 44; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/backyard-stove` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  Katharina Bauer 的话很简短：名单不能批。Josef Bauer 把表格折好，转头去搬砖搭灶。Franz Berg 从家里抱来一捆柴，火星溅在围裙上，拍两下就灭了。
  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1927.02** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Bauer和Josef Weiss谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef Bauer家，钥匙只在白天给。Josef Weiss说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Mitzi Winter听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef Bauer把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1927.02** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.lost-dog/lost-dog.chase-two-streets` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, animal, neighbor, injury
  那孩子一边跑一边吹口哨，狗就跟着口哨声跑。Josef Bauer追到街角时，罗盘钟正敲整点。她扶着墙喘，低头看见鞋底磨穿的洞，算着补鞋匠那里补一只要收六十赫勒。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (追狗跑得脚底发疼); cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销)_

- **1927.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Bauer说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Josef Huber没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef Bauer听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1928.01** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/cover` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Josef Bauer替Josef向Grete Stein请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Grete Stein看了Josef Bauer一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Josef在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:165; flag +covered_child_mistake; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1928.01** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/lie-that-stuck` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Klara告诉所有人Josef Bauer偷了东西。没有人找到赃物，但每个人都在传。Josef Bauer去市场时，摊主把零钱放在柜台上，不再递到手里。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.1; flag +false_accusation; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:167_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef Bauer把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Josef Bauer忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 40 -> 48; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 40 -> 38; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer把Otto Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Otto Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 30 -> 38; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef Bauer把Grete Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 27 -> 35; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1928.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Franz Stein夹着烟走到门口。Josef Bauer报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1928.05** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, rural
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Josef Bauer的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Friedrich Kral给了Josef Bauer两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Josef Bauer把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); health -0.040000000000000036 (滚筒造成的烫伤); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:166; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1928.05** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/public-doubt` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  旧事被人在街上重新提起，Josef Bauer的名字和另一件事绑在一起。有人替Josef Bauer辩解，也有人只笑了一声；Josef Bauer回家后把当天的收据一张张摊开，像在给自己准备证词。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 50 -> 58; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 40 -> 36; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003353_

- **1928.07** `pl19-bridge.public-rumor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-back-room-opens` tags: scandal, protection, network, press, media, rumor, bridge, rural
  报纸卖完的第二天，Anna Berg把Josef Bauer领进洗衣房后面的小间。桌上已经坐着门房、一位排字工和两个不肯说名字的人；他们没有问绯闻是真是假，只问Josef Bauer以后还打不打算出门。Josef Bauer说打算，Anna Berg就把一盏灯往桌中间推了推。
  _effects: state_add pl19-protected-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:168; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; reputation -0.02; flag +rumor_network; knowledge “洗衣房后间的人没有问绯闻真假，只问当事人还出不出门。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; flag +protected_scandal_active_

- **1928.07** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef Bauer在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Josef Bauer时停住。晚上，Josef Bauer把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 30 -> 40; reputation -0.02; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003356_

- **1928.12** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Otto Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Bauer把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 27 -> 30; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003335_

- **1929.01** `fm17-miracle.witness::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-light-on-the-grave` tags: faith, miracle, vision, public_scandal, witness, reputation, doubt, rural
  万灵节傍晚，Josef Bauer和Emil Weiss在墓园点蜡烛。一团淡蓝色的光在Josef Bauer母亲的墓碑上方停了几息，又慢慢散开。Emil Weiss先跪下，随后旁边几家人也跪了。没有人问Josef Bauer最近为什么被报纸写，但所有人看Josef Bauer的眼神都变了。
  _effects: state_add fm17-miracle-witness state:world-vienna-1912:206; reputation 0.04; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +fm17_light_seen; flag +fm17_miracle_witnessed; knowledge “我看见的那件事，到今天我仍说不出它是什么。”_

- **1929.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Maria Kral 2在午休时把本子摊在Josef Bauer的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef Bauer从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Maria Kral 2当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; reputation 0.04_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.03_

  Josef Bauer把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Josef Bauer这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。

  又过了一年，Josef Bauer学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Josef Bauer的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 43 -> 37; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 16; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  有人替Josef Bauer挡了一次采访，随后在走廊里提起自己儿子的学徒期满。Josef Bauer知道这不是闲聊；当天晚上，那张写着名字的纸条被压在糖罐下面，像一枚没有付清的硬币。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 15 -> 27; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 55 -> 51; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 55 -> 60; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Josef Bauer只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Josef Bauer把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Josef Bauer站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。

  Josef Bauer和Otto Stein一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Josef Bauer去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Josef Bauer先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  入冬后，Josef Bauer在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Josef Bauer低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 37 -> 47; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 16 -> 22; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed unknown -> curse; reputation -0.019999999999999997; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:pl19-protected-scandal/the-network-meets/someone-wants-a-favor` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  有人替Josef Bauer挡了一次采访，随后在走廊里提起自己儿子的学徒期满。Josef Bauer知道这不是闲聊；当天晚上，那张写着名字的纸条被压在糖罐下面，像一枚没有付清的硬币。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 27 -> 39; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 51 -> 47; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 60 -> 65; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:fm17-miracle-witness/the-story-year/story-hardens` tags: fm17-miracle-witness, faith, miracle, vision, witness, religion, reputation, doubt
  故事传到第三个人嘴里时，已经多了“白光”“香气”和“死去的人叫了她的名字”。Josef Bauer想纠正，却发现每纠正一次，别人就多记一条；到最后，连她自己都开始怀疑当时站在哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-miracle-witness.attention 45 -> 55; state_parameter fm17-miracle-witness.certainty 55 -> 50; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer把Otto Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Otto Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 38 -> 46; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 49 -> 46; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer把Grete Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 38 -> 46; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 49 -> 46; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cast-as-understudy` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Lechner让Josef Bauer站在舞台边上看完整场，说先背熟替补的台词。排练室的暖气坏了，演员们穿着大衣念对白。散场时，Helene Lechner把一本卷了角的剧本塞给Josef Bauer，说明晚还来。
  _effects: flag +cast_as_understudy; role; skill; knowledge “the understudy must know the whole second act by Friday”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.03** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-with-papers` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, reform
  火车在清晨进站，Otto Winter 2把Josef Bauer的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Josef Bauer把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。
  _effects: cash -2 (the passport fee and the first week's rent); state_add mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:242; state_add mb17-remittance-web state:world-vienna-1912:243; role; flag +mb17_admitted_at_border; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “临时居留纸要求在四十八小时内到区公所登记住址。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +mb17_newcomer_active; flag +mb17_remittance_web_active_

- **1930.09** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sell-the-horses` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, reform
  Josef Bauer把灵车和两匹马卖给了城西的殡仪馆，拿到的钱正好抵掉欠账，只剩下一盒黑手套和半瓶擦铜油。Franz Winter说这笔遗产算是结清了；Josef Bauer却总觉得，自己像替一个陌生人做了一场没有尸体的买卖。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉灵车和马匹的余款); reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; condition; flag +sold_inherited_hearse; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1930.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Josef Bauer没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1931.01** `fm17-miracle.aftermath::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-crowd-at-the-door` tags: faith, miracle, reputation, obligation, witness, community, money, reform
  门口开始出现不属于这个街区的人：有人留下蜡烛，有人留下铜币，有人把写着病名的纸折成小块塞进门缝。Grete Stein替Josef Bauer把东西收进篮子，收着收着忽然问，能不能给一小块她手帕的边角，好带回去给卧床的母亲。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-miracle-witness.attention 55 -> 75; cash 1 (门口留下的铜币); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +fm17_miracle_crowd_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef Bauer的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:136; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Josef Bauer晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Josef Bauer听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 48 -> 41; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 48 -> 56; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Josef Bauer学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Josef Bauer的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 47 -> 41; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 22 -> 18; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:public-scandal` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_tick, state:public-scandal
  Josef Bauer的「公开丑闻」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:167_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:pl19-protected-scandal/the-network-meets/network-holds` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  Josef Bauer又在后间见了那几个人。谁也没有提名字，只把下个月的班次、钥匙和该打的电话分了一遍；出门时，Josef Bauer发现自己的围巾被人重新叠过，像一件被妥善保管的证物。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 65 -> 60; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 47 -> 51; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 45 -> 43; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:fm17-miracle-witness/the-story-year/told-kindly` tags: fm17-miracle-witness, faith, miracle, vision, witness, religion, reputation, doubt
  Josef Bauer又把那件事讲了一遍，这次没有添油加醋。听的人问：“你确定不是灯影？”Josef Bauer说确定，也不确定。对方点点头，没有追问；这反而让Josef Bauer松了一口气。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-miracle-witness.attention 85 -> 90; state_parameter fm17-miracle-witness.certainty 50 -> 54; reputation 0.009999999999999995; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Josef Bauer能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 22; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 65 -> 63; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Josef Bauer把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 2; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 65 -> 69; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer被叫到学校，老师说Gustav Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef Bauer没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 54 -> 49; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 41 -> 46; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/benefit-approved` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Winter把Josef Bauer的工龄册翻到最后一页，又对照了厂里寄来的解雇单，说可以领十二周。钱不多，每周够买面包和煤。Josef Bauer在表格上按了手印，Franz Winter把一张薄薄的领取券推过来，说下周同一时间来，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Josef Bauer把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Josef Bauer没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 14; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 22 -> 23; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Josef Bauer当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。

- **1931.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef Bauer下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1931.12** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/widow-corrects` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  揭幕到一半，穿黑纱的Josef Weiss从第二排站起来，说那根火柴是她丈夫一八九三年在布尔诺登记的，专利号还刻在家里一只锡盒上。Maria Moser请她进办公室核对文件，Josef Bauer被留在展厅里，面对一队已经买票的小学生。第二天，铭牌换成了“无名热心市民”，Josef Bauer的名字被刮掉，只剩一道白痕。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:277; cash -1 (退掉车马费后的车钱); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; condition; flag +plaque_scraped_clean; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `bc18-humiliation.borrowed-speech::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/vet-report-read` tags: humiliation, public, speech, mistake, absurd, reputation, school, reform
  Emil Weiss 2把一叠稿纸放在讲台上，Josef Bauer没有看就念了起来。第一页写的是牛的胃病、饲料和胀气，Josef Bauer越念越慢，台下孩子笑得前仰后合；Emil Weiss 2却在本子上记：“内容具体，贴近生活。”散会后，Ferdinand Bauer问Josef Bauer是不是故意用兽医报告讽刺官僚，Josef Bauer说不是。
  _effects: cash 1 (学校给的演讲车马费); reputation 0.020000000000000004; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 22 -> 27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; condition; flag +read_the_vet_report; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  Josef Bauer把Otto Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Otto Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 49 -> 57; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 46 -> 43; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer把Grete Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 49 -> 57; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 46 -> 43; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Anton Huber 2夹着烟走到门口。Josef Bauer报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1932.03** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-number-wins-without-them` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, reform, medical
  Josef Bauer用了十几年同一组号码——父亲当年送奶路线的门牌号。这个月房租涨了，Josef Bauer少买了一张；开奖那天，那组号码中了头奖。Alois Weiss把报纸摊在桌上，Josef Bauer看了很久，最后去厨房把水壶坐上，水开了才发现自己没有拿杯子。
  _effects: cash -2 (the drink and the extra trip to the newsagent); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:313; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; flag +missed_the_numbers; knowledge “父亲送奶路线的门牌号中了头奖，而这一期我没有买。”; flag +fortune_reversed_

- **1932.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Josef Bauer把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999994 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1932.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Josef Bauer缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.039999999999999813_

- **1933.01** `mb17-diaspora.sunday-table::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/gossip-follows-the-letter` tags: migration, diaspora, community, food, belonging, mutual_aid, reform, medical
  有人把一封刚从故乡寄来的信读出声，读到“他在维也纳大概忘了家里”时，桌边的人都笑了。Josef Bauer也跟着笑，手却停在汤勺上；他想起自己上个月没有寄出的那张汇款单。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 14 -> 11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_diaspora_gossip_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Josef Bauer在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Josef Bauer低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 43 -> 53; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 14 -> 20; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:pl19-protected-scandal/the-network-meets/network-holds` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  Josef Bauer又在后间见了那几个人。谁也没有提名字，只把下个月的班次、钥匙和该打的电话分了一遍；出门时，Josef Bauer发现自己的围巾被人重新叠过，像一件被妥善保管的证物。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 55 -> 50; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 55 -> 59; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 41 -> 39; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `state_expire:fm17-miracle-witness` tags: fm17-miracle-witness, faith, miracle, vision, witness, religion, reputation, doubt
  Josef Bauer的「见过无法解释之事的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fm17-miracle-witness state:world-vienna-1912:206_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Josef Bauer能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 23 -> 27; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 16; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Josef Bauer把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 5 -> 7; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 67 -> 64; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `state_expire:bc18-ridiculous-fame` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  Josef Bauer的「荒唐的名声」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:277; flag -ridiculously_famous_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/the-swing-holds` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  半年里没有新的坏消息。Josef Bauer把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -20 -> -14; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 30 -> 26; cash 1 (small economies and an odd job); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer被叫到学校，老师说Gustav Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef Bauer没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 49 -> 44; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 46 -> 51; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/carried-the-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Bauer看见Emil Weiss在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Josef Bauer的腰在门框上撞了一下，Emil Weiss从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.030000000000000027 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1933.07** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/nothing-moved` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, reform, medical
  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Josef Bauer在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Franz Winter把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.009999999999999995; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”_

- **1933.08** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hand-crank` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, reform
  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Josef Bauer让Mitzi Lechner抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Mitzi Lechner的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Josef Bauer两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。
  _effects: organization_found 环城大道写字楼管理处 (other); position 电梯员 @building:person-josef-bauer-evt:world-vienna-1912:005896; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:365; cash 2 (the doctor's reward); reputation 0.03; flag +lift_operator; knowledge “电梯停在四楼半时，手动柄要一格一格放，不能一次松到底。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Otto Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Bauer把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  Mitzi Bauer 2把表从头到尾看了一遍，说“这次对了”。Josef Bauer问上次错在哪；Mitzi Bauer 2说“上次你把出生地写成了住址”。两个人都笑了一下；Josef Bauer接过盖好章的表，发现纸还是热的。

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef Bauer还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。

  工头让Josef Bauer把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Josef Bauer没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。

- **1935.01** `initiative:found_process:org-printers-union`
  Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens在Versammlungsraum der graphischen Arbeiter经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org-printers-union-1935 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org-printers-union-1935 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org-printers-union-1935 as organizer; knowledge “Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的持续运动不是一次会议”; reputation 0.04_

- **1935.02** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ask-a-neighbor-first` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer没有立刻填，拿着问话单去敲隔壁的门。开门的人比她年轻二十岁，说那年还没搬来；两个人站在楼道里把纸看了一遍，最后只确认了门牌号。回家后，Josef Bauer把问话单压在糖罐下面。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:448; flag +late_testimony_neighbor; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1935.02** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/someone-vouches` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  一个不算亲近的人替Josef Bauer说了句话：不是保证Josef Bauer已经变好，只说那天Josef Bauer确实在场、确实没有躲。话传回来时，Josef Bauer正在补一只旧口袋，针脚第一次没有歪。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 40 -> 47; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 40 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef Bauer在旧相册里翻到一张一九一二年的合影，最后一排站着一个和Josef Bauer年轻时一模一样的女人。照片背面只写着“四月，普拉特”。Franz Stein看了很久，说那个人也许是Josef Bauer的姑妈，可Josef Bauer记得姑妈那年还在乡下。
  _effects: knowledge “旧照片里有一个和本人年轻时一模一样的女人，背面写着“四月，普拉特””; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +found_a_double_in_the_old_photograph_

- **1937.03** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral.late` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, funeral, colleague, memory
  电车在路上误了半小时，Josef Bauer赶到公墓门口时，送葬的人已经散了，土坑边的花圈歪着。她在门口的签名簿上找到旧同事的名字，在旁边一笔一画写下自己，又看见Berta Hofer 2从里面出来，两人站在门柱旁说了一会儿话，各自搓着手。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1937.04** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pain-managed-at-home` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Bauer把楼梯改成一步一步上，买菜只走平路，疼得厉害就坐下来揉。Gustav Moser替Josef Bauer把煤筐拎上楼，换来一碗汤和一张旧报纸；两个人谁也没提医院。邻居把空筐靠在门边，顺手把楼梯上的冰敲掉。
  _effects: cash -4 (a cane, a hot-water bottle and tram fares to the market); health -0.010000000000000009 (chronic pain managed by rest and warmth); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; knowledge “膝盖疼的时候走平路、分几步上楼、用热水袋，比去医院的排队更管用。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1938.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Josef Bauer在Versammlungsraum der graphischen Arbeiter召集了一次小范围会议，和Alois Lechner把下一次行动、会费和分工写在纸上。
  _effects: project; historical development:initiative-org-printers-union-1935 as organizer; historical_influence; knowledge “我召集了一次关于Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbei的会议。”; reputation 0.02_

- **1938.02** `statistical_infection`
  Josef Bauer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1938.03** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef Bauer还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 9 -> 11_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008818_

- **1938.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef Bauer的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1938.04** `vienna-elder-bereavement.grave-visit/grave-visit.turn-back` tags: elderhood, bereavement, family, health, memory, housing
  面包铺的炉子就在街角，Josef Bauer买了刚出炉的一个，用纸包着，烫手。他和Ferdinand Bauer站在檐下吃完，谁也没再提墓园的事。
  _effects: food; cash -0.2 (买面包和去程车钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1938.05** `p9-gap-elder-solitude.cemetery-afternoon/cemetery-rain-short-visit` tags: elderhood, daily, solitude, memory, home, cemetery, injury
  那天下起雨，她把常春藤盆放在碑前，用抹布匆匆擦了两把，字还没看清就往回走。电车里全是湿衣服的味道，她到家时鞋帮已经湿透，把报纸塞进去吸水。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (冒雨往返墓园，鞋袜湿透); cash -0.3 (往墓园的电车票钱)_

- **1938.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef Bauer从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1938.08** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Josef Bauer当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 63 -> 73; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 11 -> 12; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008953_

- **1938.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Grete Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Bauer把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 79 -> 82; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009077_

- **1938.08** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Josef Bauer没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 10 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 73 -> 71; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009101_

- **1938.12** `vienna-life-old-age/chronic-illness` tags: elderhood, pension, health, injury
  Josef Bauer的腿在阴天肿起来，去药房买了药膏。医生说这是年轻时站得太久留下的毛病，除了休息没有别的办法。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (chronic leg pain); cash -5 (medicine); condition_

- **1938.12** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Gustav Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Bauer把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 53 -> 56; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009084_

- **1939.01** `project_progressed` tags: project
  「Josef Bauer召集的会议」继续推进了一阶段。
  _effects: project_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Josef Bauer把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 71 -> 75_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Josef Bauer把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Josef Bauer把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 38 -> 41; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 16 -> 14; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 46 -> 48; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:em18-late-bloom/late-life-year/takes-root` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  又一年过去，Josef Bauer把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Josef Bauer已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 42 -> 46; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 50 -> 47; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 61 -> 59; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer把Gustav Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Gustav Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 56 -> 64; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 53 -> 50; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:pc20-public-reversal/file-moves/a-copy-survives` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  书记员把一份抄件从抽屉底下抽出来，说原件已经调走，但这一份还在。Josef Bauer看见纸上自己的名字被红笔圈过，旁边有一行小字：此页曾由第三人核对。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 30 -> 37; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 60 -> 64; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 45 -> 42; knowledge “档案里还有一份抄件，曾经由第三人核对过。”_

- **1939.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Josef Bauer找到Grete Berg，把Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的章程和会费说清楚；对方留下地址，答应下次再来。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; historical development:initiative-org-printers-union-1935 as supporter; knowledge “我通过Josef Bauer加入了Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter”_

- **1939.04** `vienna-elder-care.union-office/sign-for-small-allowance` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Josef Bauer不想再跑，当场签字领取一次性十二克朗的补助，放弃追讨其余部分。
  _effects: cash 12 (一次性补助); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +union_fund_waived_

- **1939.05** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef Bauer还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009576_

- **1939.09** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.pension-window/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.pension-window.missing-page` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, bureaucracy, cash
  Alois Weiss 2在窗口里翻到第三遍，说Josef Bauer的工资册缺了一页，缺的那年工龄对不上，这个月只能先按低档发。窗口递出来一张纸条，要她下周带着原雇主或见证人的证明再来；Emil Weiss凑近看了一眼，说自己在同一家厂干过，可以替她说话。
  _effects: cash 4 (按低档先发的当月养老金); flag +pension_gap_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1940.01** `project_completed` tags: project
  「Josef Bauer召集的会议」完成了当前阶段，参与者留下了实际的记录和后续安排。
  _effects: project_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer被叫到学校，老师说Otto Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef Bauer没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 77 -> 72; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  有人在报纸的社交栏里看见Josef Bauer的名字，连旧住址都写对了。第二天，杂货店老板多问了一句，Josef Bauer笑着把话岔开；回家后，Josef Bauer把窗帘拉上，又打开。

- **1941.05** `sm18-legend.well-light::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-light-comes-back` tags: legend, well, light, neighborhood, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把镜片扔掉以后，第二个星期井口又亮了。这次碎的是另一块，边缘还带着新茬。Emil Weiss说别再去了；Josef Bauer站在院门口看了很久，直到那点白光被雾吞掉。
  _effects: knowledge “井口又出现了一块新的碎镜片，边缘带着新茬”; flag +the_well_light_came_back_

  Mitzi Bauer 2把铜板在桌上排成一列，说每月两枚，死了由会里出棺材和一顿丧饭。Josef Bauer数出第一月的钱，Mitzi Bauer 2在名字后面画了一个圈。墙上的名单已经写满了三张纸，最上面几张发了黄。
  _effects: cash -2 (丧葬会的铜板); flag +joined_burial_society; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; knowledge “the society pays for a coffin and a funeral meal”_

  葬礼在城外公墓，Josef Bauer穿着袖口磨亮的黑外套，在墓前站得比旁人久，靴底让冻土冰得发麻。回来的电车上她把旧工资册翻开，一页页看那些签名和日期，邻座的Otto Lechner 2问起，她才说起那同事当年替她顶过一班夜工。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; health -0.020000000000000018 (冻土上久站，脚趾发木); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  Alois Lechner 2说版面已经排好，改一个字要挪三栏。Josef Bauer没有争，只把报纸带回家，用铅笔在照片下面画了一道横线；Emil问那是什么意思，Josef Bauer说意思是这张照片还没有说完。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:810; flag +caption_unchanged; knowledge “照片下面被画了一道铅笔横线，意思是这张照片还没有说完。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +public_reversal_active_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Josef Bauer的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Josef Bauer家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013431_

  报纸的社会版把Josef Bauer家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.019999999999999997_

  城里的旗子又换了一次。Josef Bauer站在街口，看见第一批士兵把岗哨撤走；没有人解释接下来会怎样，但通行证不再需要每天重签了。
  _effects: state_remove occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:850; mood 0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013755_

  又过了半年，Josef Bauer把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Josef Bauer把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  炉子里的煤烧得不匀，Josef Bauer蹲下去用铁钩捅了两下，烟从缝里冒出来。Helene Winter走的时候，手里那束花用旧报纸裹着，只露出一点白。

  Josef Bauer在法庭上把上一次的日期改了回来，说那天先响的是门，不是窗。Friedrich Berg让他确认，Josef Bauer说确认；Franz Stein在旁听席上低下头，手指在膝盖上敲了两下，像在数一件只有他记得的事。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:813; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 35 -> 20; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 20 -> 30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; knowledge “那天先响的是门，不是窗；{subject}在法庭上把日期改了回来。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; flag -public_reversal_active; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1945.11** `em18-late-bloom.radio-hour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-real-singer-arrives` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, radio, comedy, mistaken_identity, absurd, vienna
  真正的歌手在节目中途推门进来，手里还拿着同一份报纸。编导的脸白了；Josef Bauer把麦克风往那边推了推，说“那就让她唱”。节目没有停，第二天报纸的广播栏里多了一行笑话，Josef Bauer的名字被拼错了。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:811; state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:812; reputation -0.02; flag +radio_exposed; knowledge “电台曾把一位六十多岁的普通人错认成旧歌手。”; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1946.03** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-repair-bench` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  修鞋铺隔壁的小门面空了出来，Josef Bauer用两克朗付了押金，把旧车床搬进去。招牌是请街角的老漆匠写的，只写了“修理”两个字；第一天来了三个人，一个修伞，一个修锁，还有一个只是进来躲雨。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:865; cash -2 (the deposit on the narrow shop); skill; reputation 0.019999999999999997; flag +shop_opened_

- **1946.05** `em18-late-bloom.encore::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sings-anyway` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, performance, memory, charity, theatre, vienna
  慈善晚会的主办人把旧海报翻出来，上面还有Josef Bauer三十年前的名字。Josef Bauer在后台试了一个音，嗓子比记忆里低，台下却已经坐满；那一晚，Josef Bauer唱了两段就停下，掌声还是响了很久。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:864; state_parameter fame.level 0.15 -> 0.2; reputation 0.04; cash 1 (the charity fee); flag +encore_performed; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1946.05** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/fall-on-stairs` tags: elderhood, health, family, social, injury
  Emil Lechner 2听见闷响从窗户探头，看见Josef Bauer坐在冰上按着手腕。面包被踩进泥雪，Emil Lechner 2跑下来捡起篮子。
  _effects: health -0.12 (楼梯滑倒手腕扭伤); cash -4 (请接骨师包扎); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1946.10** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Josef Bauer把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014446_

- **1947.01** `project_progressed` tags: project
  「Josef Bauer召集的会议」继续推进了一阶段。
  _effects: project_

- **1947.01** `em18-late-bloom.courting-family::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-child-threatens-the-will` tags: elderhood, late_life, love, family, inheritance, conflict, cohabitation, vienna
  Emil把一份旧遗嘱的抄件放在桌上，说如果Franz Stein搬进来，房子就该先写清楚归谁。Josef Bauer没有去看那张纸，只问Emil还记不记得小时候是谁在夜里背着去医院；Emil说记得，但那是两回事。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 20 -> 35; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 65 -> 57; flag +inheritance_threat; knowledge “成年子女把旧遗嘱抄件放在桌上，要求先写清楚房子归谁。”; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/threat-at-the-door` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  有人在楼下等Josef Bauer，没有报名字，只说“有些话到法庭上就不要再说了”。那人走后，楼梯间的灯泡被人拧松，Josef Bauer摸黑上了四层。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 15 -> 25; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 46 -> 43_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014938_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:hs18-blackout-rumor/official-denial` tags: hs18-blackout-rumor, rumor, blackout, fear, politics, street, place, shortage
  官方的告示贴出来了，说昨夜只是线路故障。围观的人读完，反而压低声音交换起另一件事：如果只是故障，为什么十七号的门一直没有开？
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.officialDenial true -> true; state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.credibility 65 -> 55; state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.panic 60 -> 66_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014947_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Josef Bauer把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Josef Bauer把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 43 -> 46; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 4 -> 2; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 34 -> 36; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef Bauer把Anton Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anton Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 39; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 39; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:pc20-public-reversal/file-moves/a-copy-survives` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  书记员把一份抄件从抽屉底下抽出来，说原件已经调走，但这一份还在。Josef Bauer看见纸上自己的名字被红笔圈过，旁边有一行小字：此页曾由第三人核对。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 37 -> 44; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 64 -> 68; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 42 -> 39; knowledge “档案里还有一份抄件，曾经由第三人核对过。”_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:em18-late-bloom/late-life-year/takes-root` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  又一年过去，Josef Bauer把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Josef Bauer已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 57 -> 61; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 45 -> 42; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 35 -> 33; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  又一年过去，Josef Bauer把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Josef Bauer已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 55 -> 59; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 55 -> 52; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 30 -> 28; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `agency:publish` tags: agency, agency:publish
  Josef Bauer把一篇短文交到印刷所，谈当前的争论；几天后，认识字的人在咖啡馆里传阅它。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000013_

- **1947.03** `eh19-testimony.wrong-apology::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/apologize-for-the-wrong-thing` tags: elderhood, testimony, apology, public, fame, politics, generation, history
  礼堂里坐满了学生，Helene Moser 2把话筒递给Josef Bauer，说大家只想听一句道歉。Josef Bauer对着稿子念了，念完才发现稿上写的是另一件事；Emil在最后一排站起来，又慢慢坐下。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 31 -> 26; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 10 -> 4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; knowledge “稿子上写的道歉，和老人真正想说的不是同一件事。”; flag +apologized_for_wrong_thing_

- **1947.03** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/official-return` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Josef Bauer看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 26 -> 34; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 10 -> 15_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014896_

- **1947.04** `state_reaction:em18-late-bloom/public-eyes` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  有人在报纸的社交栏里看见Josef Bauer的名字，连旧住址都写对了。第二天，杂货店老板多问了一句，Josef Bauer笑着把话岔开；回家后，Josef Bauer把窗帘拉上，又打开。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 20 -> 26; reputation 0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015178_

- **1947.06** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.landlord-heat/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.landlord-heat.more-hour` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, heating, winter
  Mitzi Bauer 2把温度计从窗台拿下来给Josef Bauer看，说煤价涨归涨，老人屋里不能只烧两小时。房东松口说早晚各多烧半小时，月租却加了一克朗二十赫勒，当场记进小本子；Maria Weiss蹲在炉边没抬头，只把敲下来的烟灰扫进铁簸箕。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (暖气加时后上涨的月租); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  Berta Huber 2说楼下的老寡妇凑不出棺材钱，会里只能先垫一半。Josef Bauer把剩下的三克朗补上，又在名单上划掉自己的名字，说今年不领了。Berta Huber 2把铜板推回来一枚，说留着自己买药。

  Josef Bauer在旧相册里翻到一张一九一二年的合影，最后一排站着一个和Josef Bauer年轻时一模一样的女人。照片背面只写着“四月，普拉特”。Grete Huber看了很久，说那个人也许是Josef Bauer的姑妈，可Josef Bauer记得姑妈那年还在乡下。
  _effects: knowledge “旧照片里有一个和本人年轻时一模一样的女人，背面写着“四月，普拉特””; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1949.01** `initiative:found_process:building:person-josef-bauer-evt:world-vienna-1912:005896`
  环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 环城大道写字楼管理处的持续运动; historical development:initiative-building:person-josef-bauer-evt:world-vienna-1912:005896-1949 as organizer; historical development:initiative-building:person-josef-bauer-evt:world-vienna-1912:005896-1949 as organizer; knowledge “环城大道写字楼管理处的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1949.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

  房东把新租约摊在柜台上，说煤价涨了，房租每月多加一克朗。Josef Bauer把账本翻到最后一页，算了一遍修一把椅子的工钱，最后还是在租约上签了字；那天下午，Josef Bauer把修理价目也重新写了一张。
  _effects: cash -3 (the rent and the new price list); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 53 -> 61; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 22 -> 19; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_paid_

  Josef Bauer出来时在门口碰见工会的老簿子，油墨味还在。签名的人不多，名字一行行往下排，Maria Stein那一行用黑框围了起来。
  _effects: cash -1 (买花和电车票); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; flag +union_book_signed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1950.09** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fill-the-form` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  区里寄来一张问话单，只有一页，问的是一九三四年住过这条街的人。Josef Bauer把老花镜从抽屉里找出来，先写自己的名字，再写“记得不清楚”；写到第三个问题，笔尖在纸上停住，窗外有人在收晾衣绳。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1046; knowledge “区里在核对一九三四年住过这条街的人。”; flag +late_testimony_questionnaire; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.12** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.old-colleague-funeral.late` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, funeral, colleague, memory
  电车在路上误了半小时，Josef Bauer赶到公墓门口时，送葬的人已经散了，土坑边的花圈歪着。她在门口的签名簿上找到旧同事的名字，在旁边一笔一画写下自己，又看见Mitzi Bauer 2从里面出来，两人站在门柱旁说了一会儿话，各自搓着手。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1951.01** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pain-electric-belt` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  报上广告说电腰带能治百病。Josef Bauer花了二十五先令买回来，绑在腰上，第一天就被烧出两排水泡；推销员已经去了下一座城市，地址是邮局信箱。他把腰带剪断，铜片和弹簧滚了一地。
  _effects: cash -25 (mail-order electric belt advertised as a cure-all); health -0.050000000000000044 (burns from an electric quack device); condition; role; knowledge “报纸上的电腰带治不了膝盖，第一天就能烧出水泡，推销员只留一个邮局信箱。”_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/someone-coaches` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  一个自称旧同事的人来家里，把一页写好的说法留在桌上，说这样对大家都好。Josef Bauer没有答应，也没有把纸扔掉；夜里，那张纸压在茶杯底下，边角被水汽卷了起来。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 30 -> 38; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 45 -> 39; knowledge “有人希望我把日期说成一九三八年，而不是一九三四年。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  又一年过去，Josef Bauer把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Josef Bauer已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。

  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 43 -> 48; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 37 -> 40; knowledge “档案里有一张纸能对上证词里的日期。”_

- **1953.07** `em18-family-archive.museum-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/donate-with-conditions` tags: elderhood, archive, memory, museum, legacy, documents, family, vienna
  Josef Bauer同意捐出信件和照片，但要求把Emil的名字写在捐赠记录上，还要留一只空箱子在家里。Berta Kral说空箱子不能入藏，Josef Bauer说那就放在门厅，谁来了都能看见。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.access 15 -> 10; state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 13 -> 21; cash 1 (a small donation grant); reputation 0.03999999999999998; flag +archive_donated; knowledge “捐赠记录上写了孩子的名字，门厅里留了一只空箱子。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

- **1954.01** `em18-family-archive.birth-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-clerk-finds-it` tags: elderhood, archive, documents, pension, bureaucracy, identity, family, vienna
  养老金办事处要一张出生证明，Josef Bauer回家翻纸箱。Berta Kral在窗口等到快下班，终于看见Josef Bauer把一张发脆的洗礼证递进来；证上的名字拼错了一个字母，但印章还清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 27 -> 31; state_parameter em18-family-archive.access 1 -> 0; cash 1 (the pension payment went through); flag +birth_certificate_found; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “洗礼证上的名字拼错了一个字母，但印章还清楚。”_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/document-surfaces` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  一只旧信封从档案馆退回来，里面有一张当年盖过章的收据。日期和Josef Bauer记得的差了一天；Josef Bauer把收据放在窗边，看了很久，最后没有改口。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 48 -> 53; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 44 -> 47; knowledge “档案里有一张纸能对上证词里的日期。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020288_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Josef Bauer把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Josef Bauer把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 55 -> 58; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 53 -> 51; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 44 -> 46; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 42; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 39; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  又一年过去，Josef Bauer从柜顶取下一只纸箱，里面的信封按年份捆着，绳子一碰就碎。Josef Bauer把出生证、地契和一张没寄出的明信片分开，桌上多出三小堆纸；最小的那一堆，Josef Bauer没有让别人看。

- **1954.04** `em18-family-archive.grandchild-photo::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-other-name` tags: elderhood, archive, memory, grandchild, photograph, family, secret, vienna
  Klara带着女儿来看Josef Bauer，小姑娘从箱子里翻出一张洗衣房的合影，背面用铅笔写着一个不是祖母的名字。Josef Bauer看了很久，说那是当年一起做工的同伴；Klara把照片翻过来，发现那个名字的笔迹和Josef Bauer平时记账的字一模一样。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.secrets 55 -> 60; state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 31 -> 29; flag +grandchild_found_photo; knowledge “洗衣房合影背面有一个不是祖母的名字，笔迹却和老人平时记账的字一样。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  炉子里的煤烧得不匀，Josef Bauer蹲下去用铁钩捅了两下，烟从缝里冒出来。Mitzi Bauer 2走的时候，手里那束花用旧报纸裹着，只露出一点白。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (托人买的一束花); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1957.01** `statistical_death/old_age` tags: death
  Josef Bauer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的床上安静地停止呼吸。桌上还放着没吃完的药和一杯水，邻居早上来敲门时才发现。Gustav Moser 2在场。
  _effects: death (老年衰竭)_
